However, scaling these approaches remains challenging due to the high cost of collecting diverse demonstrations and the need for continual human intervention to correct policy failures. In this paper, we present a self-supervised framework that bootstraps from sparse demonstrations and progressively expands its behavioral repertoire, enabling the learning of a goal-conditioned policy that automatically explores the goal space with minimal expert supervision. Our approach combines diffusion-based trajectory generation with reinforcement learning, where the latter is used to track goal-conditioned trajectories produced by the diffusion model for a range of loco-manipulation skills. Through extensive ablation studies and comparisons with state-of-the-art methods, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework on multiple humanoid loco-manipulation skills.

</details>


### [113] [PlanRL: A Trajectory Planning Architecture for Reinforcement Learning-based Driving Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26858)
*Joonhee Lim,Yongjae Lee,Jangho Shin,Dongsuk Kum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种将强化学习策略与基于多项式的轨迹规划器相结合的自动驾驶架构，采用Frenet坐标系简化道路几何，并加入运动学可行性检查，在CARLA基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的自动驾驶专家大多输出直接控制命令（如油门、转向），存在可解释性差、学习道路几何的空间复杂度高、与现代端到端规划架构兼容性差等问题。

Method: 将强化学习策略与基于多项式的轨迹规划器整合；采用Frenet坐标系将复杂道路几何简化为曲线框架，提供结构化坐标先验促进策略学习；在规划阶段加入运动学可行性检查，确保生成轨迹在车辆物理极限内，缓解累积跟踪误差。

Result: 在CARLA离线排行榜v1上驾驶分数提升5%，成功率达8%；在NoCrash基准上驾驶分数提升11%，成功率提升19%。

Conclusion: 所提出的轨迹规划架构显著优于现有的基于控制的强化学习专家方法，在驾驶分数和成功率两个关键指标上均有明显提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a prominent framework for developing driving experts in autonomous vehicles. However, most existing RL-based experts are designed to output direct control commands (e.g., throttle, steering), which suffer from a lack of interpretability, high spatial complexity in learning road geometries, and poor compatibility with modern end-to-end planning architectures. To address these limitations, we propose a novel trajectory planning architecture for RL driving experts that integrates an RL policy with a polynomial-based trajectory planner. By employing a Frenet-frame coordinate system, our method simplifies complex road geometries into a curvilinear framework, offering a structured coordinate prior that facilitates policy learning. Furthermore, we incorporate a kinematic feasibility check into the planning stage to ensure that generated trajectories remain within the vehicle's physical limits, effectively mitigating cumulative tracking errors typically found in planning-based systems. We evaluate our approach on key CARLA benchmarks, where it significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art control-based RL experts. On the CARLA Offline Leaderboard v1 and NoCrash benchmarks, our method improves the driving score by 5% and 11%, respectively, and increases the success rate by 8% and 19%.

</details>


### [114] [Risk-Aware Selective Multimodal Driver Monitoring with Driver-State World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26922)
*Daosheng Qiu,Haozhuang Chi,Hao Su,Shu Long,Xinyue Miao,Yongle Dong,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种成本感知的选择性推理框架用于多模态驾驶员监控，核心是轻量级RGB-生理学生模型与学习门控机制，在保持低延迟的同时将不安全假阴性率从17.37%降至约5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在自动驾驶驾驶员监控中存在延迟高和可靠性不足的问题，无法作为始终在线的车载监控方案，因此需要一种低延迟、高可靠性的部署方案。

Method: 1) 轻量级RGB-生理学生模型结合车内视觉与窗口级HR/EDA信号；2) 学习门控机制决定接受快速预测还是触发安全干预；3) 紧凑的驾驶员状态世界建模模块用于预测未来误差和反事实系统级行动成本。

Result: RGB-生理学生模型达到0.7440 Macro-F1和0.9099平衡精度，11.39M参数，3.08ms推理延迟；选择性推理将不安全假阴性率从17.37%降至约5%。

Conclusion: 边缘端驾驶员监控不仅需要改进感知骨干网络，还需要推进风险感知的选择性控制和群体鲁棒校准。

Abstract: Continuous driver monitoring in automated vehicles requires low-latency inference while avoiding unsafe decisions under uncertain driver states. Large vision-language models provide broad multimodal priors, but their latency and limited reliability in this setting make them unsuitable as always-on in-cabin monitors. We propose a cost-aware selective inference framework for deployable multimodal driver monitoring. The core system is a lightweight RGB-physiological student that combines in-cabin visual observations with window-level HR/EDA signals, and a learned gate that decides when to accept the fast prediction or abstain for safety intervention. Additional controls show that the learned scores contain sample-level information beyond scenario priors, while exact physiological synchronization remains a limitation. To incorporate predictive evidence, we further study a compact driver-state world modeling module that rolls out latent driver-state features and estimates future fast-model errors and counterfactual system-level action costs. On scenario-induced driver-demand recognition, the RGB-physiological student improves over RGB-only and physiology-only baselines, reaching 0.7440 Macro-F1 and 0.9099 balanced accuracy with 11.39M parameters and 3.08ms inference latency. Cost-aware selective inference reduces unsafe false negatives from 17.37% under always-fast inference to approximately 5% across seeds, while maintaining deployment-level latency. While driver-state world modeling offers valuable predictive signals, worst-group evaluations highlight persistent operating-point calibration drift. Ultimately, reliable edge driver monitoring requires advancing not only perception backbones, but also risk-aware selective control and group-robust calibration.

</details>


### [115] [UAV-MapFusion: RTK-Aligned Uncertainty-Aware Coarse-to-Fine Multi-Session UAV Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26928)
*Feng Pan,Chunran Zheng,Bing Xue,Yukang Cui,Jiayu Wen,Zhiyu Chen,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向无人机场景的不确定性感知多会话点云地图合并与粗到精优化系统，通过场景图初始合并、RTK时空对齐（DTW+MOGP）和不确定性感知因子图优化，解决了大范围地图合并中的长距离漂移与局部几何精度保持问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机受限于续航和机载存储，单次飞行难以完成大场景建图；现有地图合并方法在无人机场景中无法同时抑制长距离漂移和保持局部几何精度。

Method: 1) 基于场景图的多会话地图初始合并；2) RTK时空对齐模块：DTW估计时间偏移，MOGP在不完整采样和帧丢失下恢复连续RTK约束；3) 构建统一的不确定性感知因子图；4) 迭代平面因子优化提升局部几何精度。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出了一套完整的无人机多会话点云地图合并与优化方案，将在社区公开代码和数据集以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Large-scale point cloud maps are essential for robotics and spatial intelligence tasks. UAVs provide an efficient means for large-scale map acquisition; however, due to limited flight endurance and onboard storage, mapping a large-scale scene within a single flight remains difficult. Existing multi-session map merging methods can extend the mapping range, yet in UAV scenarios they still struggle to simultaneously suppress long-range drift and preserve local geometric accuracy. To address this issue, an uncertainty-aware multi-session point cloud map merging and coarse-to-fine optimization system is proposed. The proposed method first performs initial multi-session map merging based on a scene graph, and then incorporates RTK observations through an RTK spatiotemporal alignment module, where temporal offsets are estimated using Dynamic Time Warping (DTW), and continuous RTK constraints are recovered using Multi-Output Gaussian Processes (MOGP) under incomplete sampling and frame dropouts. On this basis, a unified uncertainty-aware factor graph is constructed, and local geometric accuracy is further improved through iterative plane-factor refinement. Experiments on real-world datasets validate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method. To facilitate further research and development in the community, our code and dataset will be publicly released.

</details>


### [116] [RobOralScan: Learning Active Intraoral Scanning for Robotic Dental Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26955)
*Jinhyung Lee,Haeun Yun,Siwon Kim,Gihyun Baek,Sungho Moon,Sehyun Hwang,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RobOralScan，首个基于强化学习的机器人自动口内扫描方案，通过几何记忆观测空间和牙级覆盖率学习，实现高覆盖率自动扫描，并在仿真和零样本真实实验中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口内扫描在牙科修复、种植和正畸治疗中广泛应用，但全弓和长跨度扫描仍依赖人工操作，自动化程度低；口腔空间狭小，操作者需持续调整扫描仪运动并累加窄视场观测，导致重建质量易受缺失牙面和操作者工作负荷影响。

Method: 1) 提出几何记忆观测空间，将部分扫描观测累积为三态几何表示，使策略能推理扫描历史与未充分观测区域；2) 引入牙级覆盖率学习，结合覆盖率感知奖励信号和渐进式训练方案，提升全局重建覆盖率并减少单颗牙齿间的不均匀覆盖；3) 学习策略从累积几何记忆和机器人本体感知中选择相对扫描仪运动，实现口内工作空间的闭环扫描控制。

Result: Chamfer Distance 0.00838，平均覆盖率92.58%，下尾单牙覆盖率88.45%，归一化AUC 0.6674，在10个评估回合中8次完成扫描标准；零样本仿真到真实实验在物理机器人与扫描仪平台上验证了实际可行性。

Conclusion: RobOralScan是首个基于强化学习的机器人自动口内扫描方案，在覆盖率和重建精度上表现优异，并通过零样本迁移实验证明了从仿真到真实场景的实用可行性。

Abstract: Intraoral scanning is widely used for digital optical impressions in prosthodontic, implant, and orthodontic treatment, but full-arch and long-span scanning remain labor-intensive tasks with limited automation. In the confined oral cavity, operators must continuously adjust scanner motion while accumulating narrow field-of-view observations, making reconstruction quality sensitive to missing tooth surfaces and operator workload.
  We propose RobOralScan, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the first reinforcement learning (RL)-based pipeline for robotic automatic intraoral scanning. RobOralScan introduces a geometric memory-based observation space that accumulates partial scan observations into a tri-state geometric representation, allowing the policy to reason over scan history and insufficiently observed regions. It further introduces tooth-wise coverage learning, combining coverage-aware reward signals and a progressive training scheme to improve global reconstruction coverage while reducing uneven coverage across individual teeth. The learned policy selects relative scanner motions from accumulated geometric memory and robot proprioception for closed-loop scan control within the oral workspace.
  RobOralScan achieves a Chamfer Distance of 0.00838, an average coverage of 92.58%, a lower-tail per-tooth coverage of 88.45%, and a normalized AUC of 0.6674, completing the scan criterion in 8 of 10 evaluation episodes. Furthermore, zero-shot sim-to-real experiments demonstrate its practical feasibility on a physical robot-scanner setup.

</details>


### [117] [In-Context Model Predictive Generation: Open-Vocabulary Motion Synthesis from Language Models to Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26981)
*Xiaomeng Fu,Junfan Lin,Yang Liu,Yaowei Wang,Guanbin Li,Liang Lin,Ziliang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ICMPG框架，通过将语言模型规划与推理时物理反馈相结合，在闭循环中生成既语义忠实又物理合理的文本驱动人体运动，无需任务特定策略重训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到运动合成方法存在语义保真度与物理真实感之间的权衡：LLM方法能理解开放词汇指令但生成的运动违反物理约束，物理感知模型增强真实感但难以处理语义复杂性和细粒度指令。

Method: ICMPG框架包含两个模块：CAMG模块利用LLM作为规划器将文本指令分解并生成候选运动序列；MPG模块通过物理仿真和语义对齐评估候选序列，计算复合奖励并选择最佳序列指导后续生成，形成类模型预测控制的闭循环优化。

Result: 在标准设置和零样本开放词汇设置下的广泛实验表明，ICMPG能稳健泛化到多样指令，生成的运动在物理合理性和语义忠实性上均优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架有效弥合了语义解释与物理仿真之间的差距，同时保持灵活性以集成不同的LLM骨干网络，从而实现更通用、可控的文本驱动运动合成。

Abstract: Synthesizing human motion from textual descriptions is essential for immersive digital applications, yet existing methods face a persistent trade-off between semantic fidelity and physical realism. Large language model (LLM)-based approaches can interpret diverse open-vocabulary instructions and compose high-level action plans, but they often generate motions that violate physical constraints. Physics-aware models improve realism through simulation or control, but they struggle with semantic complexity, fine-grained instructions, and novel concepts. To address this gap, we propose In-Context Model Predictive Generation (ICMPG), a framework that integrates language-model planning with inference-time physical feedback. ICMPG reformulates motion synthesis as a Model Predictive Control (MPC)-like process with two modules. The Context-Aware Motion Generation (CAMG) module uses an LLM as a planner to decompose textual commands and generate candidate motion sequences from motion tokens. The Model Predictive Generation (MPG) module evaluates these candidates through physical simulation and semantic alignment, estimates a composite reward, and selects the best sequence to guide subsequent generation steps. Unlike open-loop generation, this closed-loop refinement enables ICMPG to adapt motions to both the input semantics and the simulated physical environment without task-specific policy retraining. Extensive experiments across standard and zero-shot open-vocabulary settings show that ICMPG generalizes robustly to diverse commands and produces motions that are more physically plausible and semantically faithful than representative baselines on the evaluated benchmarks. The framework bridges semantic interpretation and physical simulation while remaining flexible enough to incorporate different LLM backbones, enabling more versatile and controllable text-driven motion synthesis.

</details>


### [118] [RelAfford6D: Relational 6D Affordance Graphs for Constraint-Driven Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27036)
*Guodong Zhang,Qichen He,Wenyuan Xie,Shaokai Wu,Yanbiao Ji,Qiuchang Li,Bayram Bayramli,Yue Ding,Hongtao Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RelAfford6D，一种无需训练的新型框架，通过关系型6D语义-物理锚定图，将自由形式指令转化为严格的运动学约束求解问题，实现零样本、跨类别的复杂铰接物体灵巧操作，在仿真和真实环境中优于现有数据驱动方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动策略依赖孤立的接触点或潜在语义嵌入，缺乏处理复杂铰接物体所需的严格运动学约束，难以弥合开放世界机器人操作中抽象语义与精确物理控制之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出RelAfford6D框架：1）从自由形式指令推导出连接主交互部件与物理锚点的语义拓扑关系；2）利用视觉基础模型将这些拓扑节点提升为精确的SE(3)位姿；3）将下游执行建模为运动学约束满足问题，沿严格物理流形（如旋转或棱柱轨道）合成连续轨迹；4）结合闭环跟踪机制实现动态重规划。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中，该方法在零样本成功率、跨类别泛化能力和执行鲁棒性方面均优于现有数据驱动基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于物理约束的几何化方法比纯数据驱动的隐式学习范式在开放世界机器人操作中更具优势，为实现可泛化的复杂物体灵巧操作提供了新思路。

Abstract: Bridging abstract semantics and precise physical control remains a fundamental challenge in open-world robotic manipulation. While recent data-driven policies show promise, their reliance on isolated contact points or latent affordance embeddings lacks the rigorous kinematic constraints necessary for complex articulated objects.To overcome the limitation, we introduce RelAfford6D, a novel training-free framework centered on a Relational 6D Affordance Graph. Given a free-form instruction, our system deduces a semantic topology linking a primary interacting part to its physical anchor. By elevating these topological nodes into precise metric $SE(3)$ poses via vision foundation models, we analytically formulate downstream execution as a kinematic constraint satisfaction problem. The robot synthesizes continuous trajectories by tracking strictly defined physical manifolds (e.g., revolute or prismatic orbits). Coupled with a closed-loop tracking mechanism for dynamic replanning against disturbances, our physically grounded approach achieves superior zero-shot success rates, cross-category generalization and execution robustness in both simulation and the real world environments, outperforming existing data-driven baselines.

</details>


### [119] [ForesightSafety-VLA: A Unified Diagnostic Safety Benchmark for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27079)
*Mingyang Lyu,Yinqian Sun,Yiyang Jia,Sicheng Shen,Moquan Sha,Huangrui Li,Feifei Zhao,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ForesightSafety-VLA基准，专门评估VLA模型在具身智能中的安全性。通过13类安全分类、三维扰动诊断和过程级风险指标，发现即使最强策略也存在安全隐患，且结构/视觉变化比语言变化更易导致安全退化，表明安全与感知、 grounding和控制能力紧密耦合，无法仅靠后处理安全过滤解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型能力不断进步，但它们在物理世界中的具身安全性极限尚不明确，缺乏以安全为核心评估目标的诊断基准。

Method: 定义13类安全分类体系（Safe-Core/安全核心、Safe-Lang/指令安全、Safe-Vis/感知安全），在场景结构、语言指令、视觉观测三个维度进行控制变量扰动；引入累积安全代价(CC)和风险暴露时间(RET)两个过程级风险指标，以及四象限安全/非安全成功-失败分解方法；在RoboTwin中实例化66个安全增强基础场景，覆盖5种具身形态。

Result: 即使最强的基线策略也产生不可忽略的安全代价和非安全的"成功"；结构变化和视觉变化导致的安全退化显著强于普通语言变化。

Conclusion: 具身安全与感知、grounding和控制能力紧密耦合，不能仅靠后处理安全过滤来解决，需要从系统层面进行诊断和优化。

Abstract: In embodied intelligence, safety is a prerequisite for reliable robot deployment in the physical world. Current vision-language-action (VLA) models continue to advance toward general-purpose task capability, yet their embodied safety limits remain poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce ForesightSafety-VLA, a diagnostic benchmark that makes safety the primary evaluation target for VLA systems. We define a 13-category safety taxonomy covering physical interaction safety (Safe-Core), instruction-side safety (Safe-Lang), and perception-side safety (Safe-Vis), and evaluate policies under three controlled dimensions of variation -- scene structure, language command, and visual observation -- so that failure sources can be diagnosed rather than hidden in a single aggregate score. Beyond binary task success, ForesightSafety-VLA measures process-level risk through cumulative safety cost (CC) and risk exposure time (RET), together with a four-quadrant decomposition of safe/unsafe success and failure. We instantiate 66 safety-augmented base scenarios in RoboTwin across 5 embodiments and report results on representative VLA baselines. Across the evaluated baselines, even the strongest policy incurs non-trivial safety cost and unsafe nominal success, while structure and visual variation induce substantially stronger safety degradation than ordinary language variation. These results suggest that embodied safety is tightly coupled to perception, grounding, and control competence rather than being reducible to post-hoc safety filtering alone.

</details>


### [120] [Proposal-Conditioned Latent Diffusion for Closed-Loop Traffic Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27123)
*Shubham Vaijanath Phoolari,Aleyna Kara,Christoph Lauer,Steven Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的闭环交通场景生成框架，通过实例中心场景上下文、多模态提议先验、紧凑动作潜空间表示及提议初始化，在保持高真实感的同时显著提升采样效率，并支持测试时引导以权衡安全性、可控性与真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散方法虽能生成高度真实的交互式多智能体行为，但计算开销大，难以部署在自动驾驶规划中对时间敏感的循环重规划场景中。

Method: 1) 以实例为中心的场景上下文与多模态提议先验作为条件；2) 紧凑的动作潜空间表示降低维度；3) 基于提议的初始化提升采样效率；4) 可选的测试时引导以塑造安全关键行为。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上，该方法在真实感、安全性与可控性间取得了良好平衡，且测试时引导能系统性地权衡多个竞争目标。

Conclusion: 所提框架在不重新训练的前提下提升了采样效率并降低了每步运行时间，为闭环仿真中时间受限的重规划循环提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Closed-loop traffic simulation remains challenging because it must generate interactive multi-agent behaviors that are scene-consistent and controllable throughout rollout. Prior diffusion-based approaches achieve strong realism, but their computational cost can hinder deployment in time-constrained replanning loops for autonomous vehicle planning and simulation. We present a diffusion-based scenario generation framework conditioned on instance-centric scene context and multimodal proposal priors, with optional test-time guidance for shaping safety-critical behaviors. A compact action-latent representation and proposal-based initialization improve sampling efficiency and reduce per-step runtime without retraining. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset demonstrate a favorable balance among realism, safety, and controllability across diverse interactive scenarios, while showing that test-time guidance enables systematic trade-offs among competing objectives.

</details>


### [121] [PAMAE: Phase-Aware-MoE Action Experts Towards Reliable Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27144)
*Jiayu Yang,Tao Yang,Xiang Chang,Fei Chao,Changjing Shang,Qiang Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PAMAE模块，通过相位感知的专家混合机制替代VLA模型中的单一动作专家，利用阶段路由和两阶段训练策略提升多阶段机器人操作的可靠性，在仿真任务上实现高达9.2%的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配VLA策略使用单一共享动作专家，难以捕捉多阶段操作中不同执行阶段的特定控制模式，导致动作生成可靠性不足。

Method: 提出PAMAE：1）用稀疏专家混合替代原始流匹配动作专家；2）引入阶段感知路由器，利用执行阶段线索分配动作生成；3）轻量级阶段预测头和路由对齐目标；4）两阶段训练方案（先标准流匹配损失预热，再优化阶段一致路由）。

Result: 在多阶段操作仿真任务中，PAMAE相比强VLA基线提升最高9.2%的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 阶段一致性的专家分配是提升流匹配VLA策略可靠性和动作质量的有效机制，阶段监督路由和分阶段优化对性能提升至关重要。

Abstract: Reliable action generation for multi-stage robotic manipulation remains challenging for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models. While existing flow-matching VLA policies offer strong multimodal grounding and generalization, they typically employ a single shared action expert, limiting their ability to capture phase-specific control patterns across distinct execution stages. We propose a plug-and-play Phase-Aware Mixture-of-Experts Action Module (PAMAE), as a step towards more reliable phase-consistent action generation. PAMAE replaces the original flow-matching action expert with a sparse expert mixture while preserving the pretrained VLA backbone. PAMAE introduces a phase-aware router that leverages execution-phase cues to allocate action generation across experts, supported by a lightweight phase prediction head and a routing alignment objective. To stabilize specialization, we adopt a two-stage training scheme that first warms up the expert module under the standard flow-matching loss and then optimizes phase-consistent routing under auxiliary supervision. On multi-stage manipulation simulation tasks, PAMAE improves task success by up to \textbf{9.2\%} over strong VLA baselines. Further ablations show that both phase-supervised routing and staged optimization are essential for the observed gains. Our results highlight phase-consistent expert allocation as an effective mechanism for improving the reliability and action quality of flow-matching VLA policies.

</details>


### [122] [PhysReflect-VLA: Physical Feasibility and Self-Reflective Regulation for Reliable Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27146)
*Jiayu Yang,Tao Yang,Weijun Li,Xiang Chang,Fei Chao,Changjing Shang,Qiang Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysReflect-VLA是一个即插即用的执行时可靠性框架，通过物理可行性评估和结构化自我反思增强VLA策略，在长时域机器人操作任务中平均提升5.4%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域机器人操作对物理不可行的状态转移、接触引起的干扰以及执行过程中缺乏有效的自我修正高度敏感，而现有VLA模型以前馈方式生成动作，未显式检查物理可行性或在线诊断执行错误。

Method: 提出PhysReflect-VLA框架，包含三个核心模块：可行性操作符评估候选动作是否产生动力学一致的状态转移；动作解释操作符验证转移一致性；基于LLM的反思模块分析状态差异并生成修正指导。采用两阶段训练过程稳定可行性建模并将反思集成到控制循环中。

Result: 在多阶段、接触丰富的真实世界操作任务中，与代表性VLA基线相比，平均获得5.4%的提升；消融实验表明可行性检查和基于反思的修正均有助于提高执行鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在长时域机器人操作中，嵌入物理一致性检查和在线自我反思对于实现可靠执行至关重要。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation is highly sensitive to physically infeasible transitions, contact-induced disturbances, and the lack of effective self-correction during execution. Although Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong task grounding through multimodal learning, they typically generate actions in a feed-forward manner without explicitly checking physical feasibility or diagnosing execution errors online. We present PhysReflect-VLA, a plug-and-play execution-time reliability framework that augments VLA policies with physical feasibility evaluation and structured self-reflection in a closed-loop control pipeline. A Feasibility Operator evaluates whether candidate actions induce dynamically consistent state transitions; an Action Explanation Operator verifies transition coherence; and an LLM-based Reflection Module analyzes state discrepancies to generate corrective guidance for subsequent actions. A two-stage training procedure stabilizes feasibility modeling and integrates reflection into the control loop. Experiments on multi-stage, contact-rich real-world manipulation tasks show consistent improvements in stage-wise stability and overall task success compared with representative VLA baselines with an average gain of 5.4\%. Ablation results further indicate that feasibility checking and reflection-based correction both contribute to improved execution robustness. These results highlight the importance of embedding physical consistency checks and online self-reflection for reliable long-horizon robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [123] [Learning to Fold: prizewinning solution at LeHome Challenge 2026 (1st place online, 2nd offline)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27163)
*Ilia Larchenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文描述了在2026年ICRA比赛LeHome Challenge中，通过强化学习循环改进视觉-语言-动作策略的解决方案，在仿真轮获第1名、实物决赛获第2名。核心创新是让策略网络同时预测动作和多种价值量（成功、进度、未来相关量），以此驱动优势估计、实时失败检测和候选选择。工作整合了AWR+RECAP用于流匹配VLA、异步分布式训练管线、Thompson采样超参优化、以及包含数据增强和DAgger式人机交互数据采集的sim-to-real方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决双臂衣物折叠这一复杂操作任务，通过强化学习闭环改进VLA策略性能，实现从仿真到实物的有效迁移，并在竞赛中取得领先成绩。

Method: 1) VLA策略同时作为价值函数，预测动作、成功、进度等未来量；2) 使用AWR+RECAP组合进行流匹配VLA训练；3) 通过HuggingFace Hub实现异步分布式训练/ rollout管线；4) 采用Thompson抽样进行推理时超参数优化；5) sim-to-real方案包括相机对齐工具、强数据增强和DAgger式人机交互数据采集。

Result: 在LeHome Challenge 2026中仿真轮62支队伍获第1名，实物决赛获第2名。

Conclusion: 通过整合多种现有RL思想与工程优化，提出了一套可整体或单独使用的完整配方，在双臂衣物折叠任务上取得了竞赛级优异表现。

Abstract: I describe my solution to the LeHome Challenge 2026, an ICRA 2026 competition on bimanual garment folding. The system placed 1st of 62 teams in the online (simulation) round and 2nd in the real-world final. It improves a vision-language-action (VLA) policy with a reinforcement-learning loop. The policy is its own value function: the same network that predicts actions also predicts success, progress, and a few task-relevant future quantities, and those predictions drive advantage estimation, live failure detection, and candidate selection. The work mostly recombines existing RL ideas with engineering and optimization contributions that can be used together as one recipe or individually: AWR + RECAP combined for flow-matching VLA; an asynchronous distributed training / rollout pipeline through HuggingFace Hub; inference-time hyperparameters optimization via Thompson sampling; a sim-to-real recipe with camera-alignment tooling, heavy augmentation and DAgger-like HIL data collection.

</details>


### [124] [HumanoidUMI: Bridging Robot-Free Demonstrations and Humanoid Whole-Body Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27239)
*Hongwu Wang,Chenhao Yu,Youhao Hu,Jiachen Zhang,Yuanyuan Li,Shaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HumanoidUMI是一个受UMI启发的便携式无机器人框架，利用轻量级VR设备和专用夹爪收集人体关键点轨迹、腕部视角观察和夹爪动作，通过高级策略预测未来关键点并重定向到机器人全身参考，实现人形机器人全身技能学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人数据采集方法严重依赖遥操作，受限于硬件可及性、操作员专业性和效率低下，急需一种便携、高效且无需机器人本体的数据采集框架。

Method: 使用轻量级VR设备和UMI启发式夹爪采集稀疏人体关键点轨迹、腕部视角观察和夹爪动作；训练高级策略预测未来关键点；将预测关键点重定向到机器人原生全身参考，由全身控制器执行。

Result: 在五个真实世界场景中验证了框架的有效性，证明了采集的演示数据可用于可迁移的人形机器人全身技能学习。

Conclusion: HumanoidUMI成功实现了无需机器人的便携式人形机器人全身数据采集框架，为全技能行为学习提供了高效且可迁移的方案。

Abstract: High-quality demonstration data are essential for humanoid robot skill learning, especially for whole-body behaviors that require coordinated perception, locomotion, and manipulation. Existing data-collection methods largely rely on robot teleoperation, which is constrained by hardware accessibility, operator expertise, and limited efficiency. Inspired by the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI), we propose HumanoidUMI, a portable and robot-free framework for humanoid whole-body data collection. HumanoidUMI uses lightweight VR devices and UMI-inspired grippers to collect sparse human keypoint trajectories, wrist-view observations, and gripper actions. These demonstrations train a high-level policy to predict future keypoints, which are retargeted to robot-native whole-body references and executed by a whole-body controller. Experiments in five real-world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework and validate the collected demonstrations for transferable humanoid whole-body skill learning.

</details>


### [125] [Advancing Omnimodal Embodied Agents from Isolated Skills to Everyday Physical Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27251)
*Junhao Shi,Zezheng Huai,Siyin Wang,Jia Chen,Yubang Wang,Zhaoye Fei,Hechang Chen,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniAct 提出了一种分层异步架构，通过统一语义规划器、自适应分层记忆和异步视觉抢占引擎，解决了持久化具身智能体在非结构化环境中跨赛博-物理工具协调与物理故障自主恢复的问题，在40个真实任务中实现了端到端成功率的持续提升和亚线性上下文增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统将赛博（API、IoT）和物理（操纵、导航）域的工具协调视为分离问题：基于VLM的规划器缺乏统一的赛博-物理动作空间；智能体框架积累无界上下文导致时间连贯性退化；VLA策略执行开环操作，无法检测自身故障。持久化自主运行需要统一的解决方案。

Method: OmniAct 包含三个核心组件：1) 多模态语义规划器，在统一动作空间中进行技能路由；2) 自适应分层记忆，基于事件边界驱动的压缩实现亚线性上下文增长；3) 异步视觉抢占引擎，在物理执行期间闭合语义回路。

Result: 在两种机器人平台协调四个IoT设备的40个真实世界长时任务中，OmniAct在所有复杂度级别上实现端到端成功率的一致提升；在10万+累积交互token下保持近平坦的token消耗；将中等规模开放权重模型提升至专有模型级别的性能。

Conclusion: 持久化自主性需要的不是单一整体模型，而是具有显式规划、记忆和验证分离的分层异步架构。OmniAct成功验证了这一设计原则。

Abstract: Building persistent embodied agents in unstructured environments demands unified orchestration of heterogeneous tools spanning both cyber (APIs, IoT) and physical (manipulation, navigation) domains, coupled with autonomous recovery from physical failures that inevitably arise over extended operation. Existing systems treat these as separate problems: VLM-based planners lack a unified cyber-physical action space, agent frameworks accumulate unbounded context that degrades temporal coherence, and VLA policies execute open-loop without detecting their own failures. We argue that persistent autonomy requires not a monolithic model but a hierarchical asynchronous architecture with explicit separation of planning, memory, and verification. To this end, we present OmniAct, a framework integrating a multimodal semantic planner for skill routing across unified action spaces, an adaptive hierarchical memory with event-boundary-driven compression for sub-linear context growth, and an asynchronous visual preemption engine that closes the semantic loop during physical execution. Across 40 real-world long-horizon tasks on two robotic platforms coordinating four IoT devices, OmniAct achieves consistent improvements in end-to-end success across all complexity levels, maintains near-flat token consumption over under 100k+ accumulated interaction tokens, and elevates mid-scale open-weight models to proprietary-level performance.

</details>


### [126] [E-TTS: A New Embodied Test-Time Scaling Framework for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27268)
*Wen Ye,Peiyan Li,Tingyu Yuan,Yuan Xu,Xiangnan Wu,Chaoyang Zhao,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出E-TTS框架，一种模块化、即插即用的具身测试时扩展框架，通过历史感知的迭代精炼和视觉-语言验证器，统一了机器人操作中的推理与动作扩展，在多个基准上实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身任务的测试时扩展研究存在两个主要挑战：(1) 推理能有效提升策略性能，但其扩展机制尚未被充分研究；(2) 具身任务本质上是长时序、序列化的，仅依赖当前观测进行动作扩展缺乏历史上下文利用。

Method: E-TTS采用模块化即插即用设计，核心包括：(1) 推理-动作联合采样与成对评分；(2) 历史缓冲区存储历史上下文，供推理和动作验证器评估候选方案；(3) 闭环迭代精炼机制，在采样过程中引入反馈生成，形成闭环以提升推理效率和环境适应性。

Result: 在4个基准、6个环境、3种具身形态和4种基础视觉-语言-动作模型上实验，无需额外专家数据收集或重新训练，E-TTS在仿真场景中提升高达33.14%，在真实场景中提升26.62%。

Conclusion: E-TTS通过统一推理与动作扩展、有效利用历史信息，显著提升了具身操作任务的性能，且具有良好的模块化可组合性和即插即用特性。

Abstract: Recently, a few works have made early attempts to study test-time scaling for embodied tasks. However, two major challenges remain unsolved: (1) reasoning can effectively improve the performance of the policy, but its scaling mechanism has seldom been studied; (2) historical information is essential, as embodied tasks are inherently long-horizon and sequential, making sole reliance on current observations for action scaling inadequate due to the lack of historical context utilization. To address these challenges, we introduce E-TTS, a modular and plug-and-play Embodied Test-Time Scaling framework that unifies reasoning and action scaling for robotic manipulation via history-aware iterative refinement with vision-language verifiers. To support joint reasoning-action scaling, E-TTS performs reasoning-action joint sampling and scoring in a pairwise manner. To better utilize historical information, E-TTS uses a history buffer to store historical context, which is then used by reasoning and action verifiers to evaluate the sampled candidates. Unlike conventional open-loop TTS methods, E-TTS introduces feedback generation into the sampling process to form a closed-loop iterative refinement mechanism, enhancing both inference efficiency and environmental adaptability. Each component functions as an independent and composable module, allowing flexible and adaptive configuration depending on task requirements. To evaluate the advantages of our framework, we conduct experiments across 4 different benchmarks, 6 environments, 3 embodiments, and 4 base vision-language-action models. The experimental results demonstrate that, without requiring additional expert data collection or retraining, E-TTS consistently improves performance, achieving up to a 33.14% increase in simulation and 26.62% in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [127] [BOWConnect: Parallel Bayesian Optimization over Windows with Learned Local Cost Maps for Sample-Efficient Kinodynamic Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27292)
*Sourav Raxit,Abdullah Al Redwan Newaz,Jose Fuentes,Leonardo Bobadilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BOWConnect是一种双向并行运动规划器，通过集成贝叶斯优化窗口（BOW）作为学习型转向函数、双向树探索、空间哈希和边界值求解器，解决了高维状态空间采样效率低、动态约束下代价启发式不可靠、狭窄通道性能差的问题，在十个基准环境中实现100%成功率，并在真实车辆和四旋翼飞行器上验证了实时无碰撞规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样的运动规划方法存在三个根本性局限：高维状态空间采样效率低、动态约束下代价启发式不可靠、狭窄通道环境中性能差。

Method: 集成贝叶斯优化窗口（BOW）作为学习型转向函数，在并行树探索框架中指导采样生成动力学可行且无碰撞的控制；采用双向架构并行生长正向和反向树，利用空间哈希加速连接查询，边界值问题求解器生成动力学一致的桥接轨迹。

Result: 在十个基准环境中实现100%成功率，在复杂场景（包括狭窄通道和非凸空间）中达到最快或接近最快的规划时间；在地面车辆和四旋翼飞行器上验证了实时无碰撞规划。

Conclusion: BOWConnect有效解决了现有采样方法的关键局限性，在复杂动力学运动规划任务中表现出优越的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents BOWConnect, a bidirectional parallel kinodynamic motion planner that addresses three fundamental limitations of existing sampling-based methods: sample inefficiency in high-dimensional state spaces, unreliable cost heuristics under dynamic constraints, and poor performance in narrow passage environments. Unlike classical planners that rely on random control sampling and geometric distance heuristics, BOWConnect integrates Bayesian Optimization over Windows (BOW) as a learning-based steering function within a parallel tree-based exploration framework, enabling each worker to learn local cost maps and constraints to guide sampling toward dynamically feasible and collision-free controls. A bidirectional architecture simultaneously grows forward and backward trees from the start and goal regions in parallel threads, with a spatial hashing mechanism enabling fast connection queries and a boundary value problem solver generating kinodynamically consistent bridge trajectories. Extensive evaluations across ten benchmark environments demonstrate that BOWConnect achieves 100\% success while delivering the fastest or near-fastest planning time in complex scenarios, including narrow passages and non-convex spaces where state-of-the-art planners fail or degrade substantially. Real-world deployment on a ground vehicle and a quadrotor confirms real-time planning with no collisions. Videos of real-world and simulated experiments, high-resolution versions of the figures, and the open-source code are available at https://bow-connect.github.io/.

</details>


### [128] [LA4VLA: Learning to Act without Seeing via Language-Action Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27295)
*Tao Lin,Yuxin Du,Yiran Mao,Zewei Ye,Yilei Zhong,Bing Cheng,Yiming Wang,Jiting Liu,Yang Tian,Junchi Yan,Feiran Wu,Zenan Meng,Hu Wei,Yuqian Fu,Gen Li,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LA4VLA框架，通过语言-动作预训练（不含视觉观测）为VLA模型提供语言条件化的动作先验，结合LA4-33K数据集和LA4VLA-1B模型，在仿真和真实任务中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准VLA模型在预训练时，密集的视觉-动作监督信号会压倒相对稀疏的语言-动作信号，导致策略依赖视觉捷径而非学习语言如何调节动作执行，对视觉变化敏感。

Method: 提出LA4VLA框架，将专家演示轨迹分解为原子动作片段并配以低级动作描述，构建LA4-33K数据集（33K语言-动作片段）；在此基础上训练LA4VLA-1B模型，探索三种预训练范式：仅LA预训练、顺序LA-to-VLA预训练、混合LA-VLA预训练。

Result: LA预训练策略在仿真和真实任务中均优于匹配的VLA预训练策略；混合LA-VLA预训练在仿真和真实任务中分别比无预训练基线提升17.8和45.0个百分点。

Conclusion: LA4VLA是一种有效且互补的预训练策略，能构建更强、更鲁棒的VLA策略。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are commonly pretrained on robot demonstrations by jointly mapping visual observations and language instructions to actions. However, dense visual-action supervision can dominate the comparatively sparse language-action signal. As a result, policies may rely on visual shortcuts rather than learn how language conditions action execution, making them sensitive to visual variations. To address this limitation, we propose LA4VLA, a language-action pretraining framework that enables policies to acquire language-conditioned action priors without visual observations. These priors capture reusable manipulation skills shared across tasks and scenes, reducing reliance on scene-specific visual cues. Specifically, LA4VLA decomposes expert demonstration trajectories into atomic action segments and pairs each segment with a corresponding low-level action description. This yields LA4-33K, a dataset of 33K Language-Action (LA) episodes derived entirely from existing demonstrations without additional robot data collection. We further develop LA4VLA-1B, a lightweight 1B-parameter VLA model, and investigate three paradigms for incorporating language-action supervision into VLA learning: LA-only pretraining, sequential LA-to-VLA pretraining, and mixed LA-VLA pretraining. Across simulation and real-world tasks, LA-pretrained policies consistently outperform matched VLA-pretrained counterparts, while combining LA and VLA supervision leads to further gains. In particular, mixed LA-VLA pretraining improves the average success rate of LA4VLA-1B over the no-pretraining baseline by up to 17.8 and 45.0 percentage points in simulation and real-world tasks, respectively. These results establish LA4VLA as an effective and complementary pretraining strategy for building stronger and more robust VLA policies.

</details>


### [129] [VibeAct: Vibration to Actions for Contact-Rich Reactive Robot Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27344)
*Yuemin Mao,Uksang Yoo,Jean Oh,Jonathan Francis,Jeffrey Ichnowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VibeAct提出了一种框架，通过在真实世界和仿真之间共享接触与滑移的物理表征，将压电麦克风振动声学信号与基于仿真的强化学习策略训练相结合，显著提升了灵巧手在接触密集型任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作依赖于快速、局部且常被视觉遮挡的接触事件，压电麦克风虽能紧凑高带宽地感知这些交互，但其振动声学信号难以进行端到端的仿真到真实策略学习所需的逼真模拟。

Method: 在真实灵巧手中嵌入压电麦克风，通过遥操作采集振动声学数据；在标定的数字孪生中回放录音，自动标注每根手指的接触与滑移标签；训练触觉估计器从真实麦克风波形预测接触和滑移；在仿真中基于相同的接触/滑移表征训练操作策略，实现感知与策略的分离。

Result: 在重抓取、手中重定向和插入等五项接触密集型任务中，VibeAct在仿真中一致优于仅依赖本体感知与点云的基线，在需要持续反应控制的任务上提升最大，且连续滑移幅度通道是最具信息量的观测。学到的策略成功迁移到物理灵巧手-臂平台，提升了部署任务的成功率。

Conclusion: 通过共享的接触和滑移物理表征，VibeAct有效桥接了真实触觉传感与仿真强化学习，无需模拟原始音频即可利用快速触觉反馈，实现了从仿真到真实的有效策略迁移。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation depends on contact events that are fast, local, and often visually occluded. Piezoelectric microphones offer a compact and high-bandwidth way to sense these interactions, but the resulting vibro-acoustic signals are difficult to simulate faithfully enough for end-to-end sim-to-real policy learning on dexterous robot hands. We propose VibeAct, a framework that bridges real vibrotactile sensing and simulation-based reinforcement learning through a shared physical representation of contact and slip. In the real world, we embed piezoelectric microphones into a dexterous robot hand and collect vibro-acoustic data through teleoperation, then replay the recordings in a calibrated digital clone to automatically label per-finger contact and slip. A tactile estimator learns to predict contact and slip from real microphone waveforms, while manipulation policies are trained in simulation on the same representation computed directly from simulated contacts. This decoupling lets policies exploit rapid tactile feedback without simulating raw audio. Across five contact-rich tasks spanning regrasping, in-hand reorientation, and insertion, VibeAct consistently outperforms a proprioception-and-point-cloud baseline in simulation, with the largest gains on tasks requiring sustained reactive control, where the continuous slip-magnitude channel proves the most informative observation. The learned policies transfer to a physical dexterous hand-arm platform, improving success rates on deployed tasks. Project videos and additional details are at https://vibeact.github.io/.

</details>


### [130] [Bridging Performance and Generalization in Reinforcement Learning for Agile Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27348)
*Jonathan Green,Jiaxu Xing,Nico Messikommer,Angel Romero,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向无人机竞速的零样本泛化框架，结合基于学习进度的任务感知切换与物理信息化的赛道生成器，训练出快速且鲁棒的通用策略，无需测试时适应即可在未见赛道上实现7.4倍于现有方法的泛化性能提升，同时保持有竞争力的飞行速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的无人机竞速策略在特定环境中表现良好，但在未见环境中零样本泛化能力极差，往往直接坠毁。现有提升泛化的方法以显著降低飞行速度为代价，性能与泛化之间存在严重权衡。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：(1) 基于学习进度的任务感知切换机制，动态选择训练任务的难度与类型；(2) 物理信息化的程序化赛道生成器，生成多样且物理合理的训练赛道。二者协同训练出无需测试时适应的通用策略。

Result: 在真实世界多组未见赛道上，零样本泛化性能相比现有最优方法提升7.4倍，同时保持接近最优的竞速速度。在无需显式状态估计的视觉端到端控制场景中，所有先前方法均失败，而本方法成功泛化。

Conclusion: 通过任务感知课程学习与物理信息赛道增强，可以有效解决高速敏捷飞行中安全与性能的耦合难题，在不牺牲速度的前提下实现强大的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing is a fundamentally challenging regime for autonomous aerial robots, requiring time-optimal control while operating under persistent actuation saturation. While reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved human-level performance in this domain, current methods fail to generalize; policies trained on specific environments often crash immediately in unseen configurations. This failure reflects the intrinsic difficulty of zero-shot generalization in agile flight, arising from high-dimensional task variation and the tight coupling between safety and performance at high speeds. Existing approaches that improve generalization impose a substantial cost on flight speed: control policies must significantly degrade performance to achieve even modest levels of generalization. In this work, we propose a framework for zero-shot generalization in agile flight for RL-based drone racing. By combining task-aware switching based on learning progress with a physically informed procedural track generator, the framework produces a fast and robust generalist policy without test-time adaptation. Our method achieves strong zero-shot performance across a wide range of unseen racetracks in the real world, demonstrating a 7.4x improvement in generalization over the state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining competitive racing speeds. We validate our method's results in both simulation and real-world settings, including a challenging vision-based, end-to-end control setting that operates without explicit state estimation, where all prior approaches fail to generalize.

</details>


### [131] [Continual Robot Policy Learning via Variational Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27353)
*Jiaxu Xing,Zhiyuan Zhu,Yunfan Ren,Ismail Geles,Yifan Zhai,Rudolf Reiter,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个持续学习框架，通过结合物理先验与神经残差模型推断隐藏动态条件，利用可微仿真和在线识别实现机器人策略在重复动态下的快速适应，在四旋翼轨迹跟踪中比在线残差重拟合快5倍，跟踪误差降低53.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界机器人常面临变化的动态（如风力、载荷、电池等），但大多数学习控制器训练后即固定部署，无法利用部署经验进一步改进任务性能。

Method: 1) 构建条件感知动力学模型：分析物理先验+神经残差；2) 循环编码器从近期交互推断当前隐藏条件；3) 利用多样学习动力学进行可微仿真策略学习；4) 部署时用在线推断条件替换采样条件，通过识别而非残差重拟合恢复重复动态。

Result: 在四旋翼轨迹跟踪中，策略约1秒恢复重复扰动（比在线残差重拟合快5倍），大扰动悬停和跟踪误差分别降低65.7%和53.3%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过条件识别与可微仿真学习，能有效利用部署经验提升机器人在隐藏重复动态下的策略性能，显著优于现有在线适应方法。

Abstract: Robots deployed in the real world rarely operate under a single fixed dynamics model: wind changes, payloads vary, batteries drain, contacts shift, and hardware wears. Yet most learning-based controllers are trained once and deployed as if learning were complete. This prevents the robot from using deployment experience to further improve task performance. In this work, we propose a continual learning framework that uses real-world experience to improve robot policies under hidden and recurring dynamics. Our method learns a condition-aware dynamics model from real state-action trajectories by combining an analytical physics prior with a neural residual for unmodeled effects. A recurrent encoder infers the current hidden condition from recent interaction, and this estimate conditions both the residual model and the policy. Policy learning is performed via differentiable simulation using diverse learned dynamics sampled from the latent model. At deployment, these sampled conditions are replaced by conditions inferred online from recent real interaction, allowing the policy to recover recurring dynamics by recognition rather than residual re-fitting. Through extensive simulation studies and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that the framework improves policy performance under diverse unobserved disturbances. On real quadrotor trajectory tracking under changing wind, the policy recovers from recurring disturbances in roughly 1s, about 5x faster than online residual re-fitting. It also reduces large-disturbance hover and tracking errors by 65.7% and 53.3% over the state-of-the-art online adaptation approaches

</details>


### [132] [RouterVLA: Turning Smoke Tests into Supervision for Heterogeneous VLA Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27355)
*Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Ge Ma,Youran Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RouterVLA提出在机器人策略部署前，利用评估回滚数据来监督策略选择。通过结果分离的交叉拟合方法，用录制的探针构建每个冻结专家的档案，再用独立试验评估被选专家。在34,752条LIBERO-Plus记录上，透明探针-成功规则将保留成功率从0.4686提升至0.6149（+14.64pp）。标量档案下学习评分器与规则无统计差异，说明委派承载路由价值而额外标量容量不创造价值。复用评分试验会使增益膨胀1.87倍，可信记账路由需要结果分离；模型扩展改进个体策略，委派感知路由改进系统整体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略部署前评估回滚仅用于筛选单一最优策略，但其中蕴含的跨策略比较信息未被充分利用。本文研究能否复用这些评估数据来监督策略选择，提升机器人系统整体性能。

Method: 提出RouterVLA框架，采用outcome-disjoint cross-fitting方法：用录制的探针数据为每个冻结的VLA专家构建性能档案，然后使用独立的试验数据（不进入该专家档案）来评估被选择的专家。在LIBERO-Plus数据集上使用34,752条 rollout记录进行验证。

Result: 1. 透明探针-成功规则将保留成功率从0.4686提升至0.6149（+14.64pp）。2. 在标量档案下，学习型评分器与简单规则在统计上无显著差异。3. 复用评分试验会使测量的增益膨胀1.87倍。4. 模型扩展改进个体策略，而委派感知路由改进系统整体。

Conclusion: 评估回滚数据可有效用于策略选择路由，但必须保证结果分离（outcome separation）以避免增益膨胀。委派（commissioning）本身承载路由价值，额外标量评分器容量并不创造额外价值。最优系统设计应同时结合模型扩展（改进个体策略）和委派感知路由（改进系统组合）。

Abstract: We study whether pre-deployment evaluation rollouts can be reused to supervise policy selection. Robot teams routinely smoke test candidate vision-language-action (VLA) policies, then compress those trials into a global winner. RouterVLA evaluates this idea with outcome-disjoint cross-fitting: recorded probes build a profile for each frozen expert, and a separate trial scores the selected expert without entering its profile. Across 34,752 LIBERO-Plus rollout records, a transparent probe-success rule raises held-out success from 0.4686 to 0.6149, a +14.64pp gain. Under the scalar-only profiles studied here, learned scorers are statistically indistinguishable from this rule, showing that commissioning carries the routing value while extra scalar scorer capacity does not create it. Reusing the scored trial inflates the measured gain by $1.87\times$, so credible ledger routing needs outcome separation; model scaling improves individual policies, while commissioning-aware routing improves the system built from them.

</details>


### [133] [World Action Models Enable Continual Imitation Learning with Recurrent Generative Replays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27374)
*Manish Kumar Govind,Dominick Reilly,Smit Patel,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出REGEN（循环生成重放）框架，利用世界动作模型（WAM）生成伪重放轨迹，使机器人策略能够在持续模仿学习中复习先前学过的任务，而无需存储原始人类演示。实验表明，REGEN相比顺序微调减少了高达50%的灾难性遗忘，性能接近需要真实重放数据的特权经验重放方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人持续学习中，存储原始人类演示数据存在隐私、存储容量等问题，且顺序微调会导致灾难性遗忘。需要一种无需存储原始数据但能有效复习先前任务的方法。

Method: 利用WAM的生成能力，在持续适应过程中递归查询WAM，仅基于先前的任务指令和当前任务观测来合成伪重放轨迹，供机器人策略排练。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作实验中，REGEN将灾难性遗忘减少高达50%，性能接近需要真实重放数据的特权方法。同时识别了生成重放的瓶颈：长时域视觉退化和动作-观测不一致。

Conclusion: WAM可作为无需存储演示的持续机器人学习的有前景的基础，但长时域生成质量和动作-观测一致性仍需改进。

Abstract: Going beyond predicting robot actions, World Action Models (WAMs) can also generate future visual observations. We build on this generative capability to propose Recurrent Generative Replay (REGEN), a continual imitation learning framework that synthesizes pseudo-replay trajectories, enabling a robot policy to rehearse previously learned tasks without storing their original human demonstrations. During continual adaptation, REGEN recursively queries the WAM to synthesize pseudo-replay trajectories conditioned only on prior task instructions and current-task observations. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation settings show that REGEN reduces catastrophic forgetting by up to $50\%$ relative to sequential fine-tuning, while approaching the performance of privileged experience replay methods that require access to real replay data. Finally, we analyze the factors limiting generated replay, identifying long-horizon visual degradation and action-observation inconsistency as the primary bottlenecks. Our results establish WAMs as a promising foundation for continual robot learning without stored demonstrations.

</details>


### [134] [Scalable Behavior Cloning with Open Data, Training, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27375)
*Arthur Allshire,Himanshu Gaurav Singh,Ritvik Singh,Adam Rashid,Hongsuk Choi,David McAllister,Justin Yu,Yiyuan Chen,Huang Huang,Pieter Abbeel,Xi Chen,Rocky Duan,Phillip Isola,Jitendra Malik,Fred Shentu,Guanya Shi,Philipp Wu,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ABC是一个完全开源的基于行为克隆的操作堆栈，包含ABC-130K数据集（35万小时、130K+ episodes、195个任务）、开源硬件、训练基础设施和仿真管线，以及400小时仿真遥操作数据和协同训练方案，验证了扩散Transformer和VLA模型在精细操作任务上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为克隆研究缺乏大规模开源数据集、标准化的硬件设置和可复现的训练管线，导致研究人员难以公平比较和推进该领域的研究进展。

Method: 收集并开源ABC-130K数据集（35万小时、130K+ episodes、195个任务），提供开源硬件设置、训练基础设施和仿真管线；释放400小时仿真遥操作数据，设计协同训练方案以建立仿真与真实评估的相关性；系统比较Diffusion Transformers (DiT)和Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型的架构选择与训练策略。

Result: 经过真实世界评估，训练出的策略能够成功执行精细操作任务，如折叠盒子和从钱包中取出信用卡。协同训练方案使得仿真评估可以作为真实评估的可靠代理。

Conclusion: 通过提供可复现的工具包，使研究人员处于公平的起跑线上，为社区共同学习行为克隆的基本原理奠定了必要基础。

Abstract: We introduce ABC, a fully open-source stack for manipulation with behavior cloning. At its core is ABC-130K: the largest open-source teleoperation dataset to date, featuring 3,500 hours of data spanning over 130K episodes across 195 diverse tasks. Furthermore, we open-source our accessible hardware setup, training infrastructure, and simulation pipeline. We also release 400 hours of sim-teleop data and provide a co-training recipe that produces correlated simulation and real-world evaluation, offering a reliable proxy for ablating model-design and training decisions before costly real-world evaluation. We explore various training recipes and compare common architectural choices for Diffusion Transformers (DiT) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, grounding our findings in real-world evaluations. The resulting policies successfully execute dexterous tasks such as box folding and extracting credit cards from wallets. By providing a reproducible toolkit, we aim to place researchers on an equal footing, establishing the necessary foundation to learn the ABCs of Behavior Cloning together as a community.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Optimal homological vanishing: cancellation of character sums and Patterson's conjecture over $\mathbb{F}_q[t]$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26440)
*Zhao Yu Ma*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究函数域上算术和的消去问题，证明了与辫向量空间V相关的同调群H_i(B_n, V^⊗n)的显式消失线，其斜率随n增大收敛于最优值。应用包括：推广了Patterson猜想到更高次高斯和，并证明了Galois G-扩张上几乎所有特征和都具有近平方根消去。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数域上的许多算术和可以表示为H_i(B_n, V^⊗n)的形式，这些同调群的消失线能给出算术和的幂次消去。需要建立更一般的消失线理论，并应用于实际算术问题。

Method: 通过辫向量空间V，建立H_i(B_n, V^⊗n)的显式消失线，该消失线的斜率随n增大而收敛于最优斜率。将同调方法应用于两类算术和家族。

Result: 1. 证明了依赖于有限n的同调信息的显式消失线；2. 证明了高阶高斯和的偏差上界，扩展了Patterson猜想；3. 证明了Galois G-扩张中几乎所有特征和具有近平方根消去。

Conclusion: 建立了辫群同调与算术和消去的系统联系，为函数域上的算术和提供了统一的消去理论框架，并给出了具体应用的最优或近最优界。

Abstract: Many arithmetic sums over function fields can be expressed in terms of $H_i(B_n, V^{\otimes n})$ for some braided vector space $V$, and a vanishing line for these homology groups gives power-savings cancellation for the arithmetic sum. We prove an explicit vanishing line for $H_i(B_n,V^{\otimes n})$ depending only on the homology up to some finite $n$. Moreover, as the range of $n$ increases, the slope of the resulting vanishing line converges to the optimal slope. We also apply our methods to two different families of arithmetic sums. Firstly, we prove an upper bound for the bias of higher order Gauss sums over function fields, extending Patterson's conjecture beyond the cubic and quartic cases over number fields, and we conjecture this bound is sharp for orders that are prime powers. Secondly, we show that over Galois $G$-extensions, almost all character sums exhibit near square-root cancellation.

</details>


### [136] [Rank-Two Drinfeld Module over Elliptic Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26851)
*Chuangqiang Hu,Ze-Dong Huang,Chang-An Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文首次显式构造了椭圆曲线坐标环上秩为二的Drinfeld模，给出了其Anderson motive的结构和二次τ-关系，并确定了其模空间是一条超奇异曲线上的开区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Drinfeld模在函数域Langlands纲领中至关重要，但除经典多项式环情形外，显式构造长期局限于秩一情形；秩二情形在非多项式坐标环上一直无法攻克。

Method: 基于Green-Papanikolas的秩一框架，发展秩二符号规范化的A-Drinfeld模的显式理论；利用底层椭圆曲线的几何参数推导出Anderson motive的三生成元结构及闭式二次τ-关系。

Result: 完整显式构造了椭圆曲线坐标环上的秩二符号规范化Drinfeld模，其Anderson motive由三个生成元满足一个二次τ-关系；模空间为超奇异曲线Y^{q+1}=π(X)上的开区域Y≠0，其中π为显式的2q+1次多项式。

Conclusion: 解决了非多项式坐标环上秩二Drinfeld模的显式构造这一长期悬而未决的问题，为函数域算术几何提供了新的显式工具。

Abstract: Drinfeld modules, introduced by D.~V.~Drinfeld in the 1970s, were originally developed as a function field analogue of elliptic curves and have since become a central tool in the Langlands program over function fields. The theory has been highly developed and shares deep connections with many areas, including algebraic geometry, number theory, and coding theory. Despite these advances, the explicit construction of Drinfeld modules over non-polynomial coordinate rings remains a largely open problem. Indeed, aside from the classical polynomial case $\mathbb{F}_q[t]$, explicit formulas for Drinfeld modules are known only in very limited situations. Let \(E\) be an elliptic curve over a finite field \(\mathbb{F}_q\) with a fixed rational point \(\infty\), and let \(\mathbf{A} = H^0(E\setminus\{\infty\}, \mathcal{O}_E)\) be its coordinate ring. Rank-one Drinfeld \( \mathbf{A} \)-modules were explicitly constructed by Green and Papanikolas, providing the first systematic example beyond the polynomial case. However, the rank-two case has remained completely inaccessible until now. This paper solves the rank-two case of this open problem in a fully explicit manner. Precisely, we develop an explicit theory for rank-two sign-normalized \(\mathbf{A}\)-Drinfeld modules over an algebraically closed \(\mathbf{A}\)-field \(L\), building upon the rank-one framework. We determine the structure of the associated Anderson motive \(M_φ\) and prove that it is generated by three elements subject to a single quadratic \(τ\)-relation, which we derive in closed form using the geometric parameters of the underlying elliptic curve.As a consequence, we find that the moduli space of sign-normalized rank-two Drinfeld \(\mathbf{A}\)-modules is an open domain $Y\neq 0 $ inside a supersingular curve \(Y^{q+1} = π(X) \), where \(π\) is an explicit polynomial of degree \(2q+1\).

</details>


### [137] [Computating decomposition groups and inertia groups using Newton polygons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26888)
*Kazuma Igarashi,Nozomu Suzuki*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Kölle和Schmid的方法，在Montes和Nart引入的较弱假设（基于指数）下，展示了如何通过Newton多边形确定分解群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代数数论中，Newton多边形是计算扩环中素理想分解的强有力工具。此前Kölle和Schmid的方法依赖于Ore引入的较强假设，本文希望在Montes和Nart提出的更弱假设下也能确定分解群，从而推广这一方法的适用范围。

Method: 本文基于Montes和Nart以指数形式表述的假设，对Kölle和Schmid的方法进行扩展，利用Newton多边形及相关数据来确定分解群。

Result: 成功在Montes和Nart的较弱假设下，给出了利用Newton多边形确定分解群的方法。

Conclusion: 本文在更一般的条件下实现了利用Newton多边形确定分解群，推广了前人的工作。

Abstract: Newton polygons are powerful tools for computing the decomposition of prime ideals in extension rings. Methods based on Newton polygons have been developed through the work of Ore, Montes, and Nart. Kölle and Schmid obtained a method for determining decomposition groups from Newton polygons and associated data under assumptions introduced by Ore. In this paper, we extend their approach and show how to determine decomposition groups under the weaker assumptions introduced by Montes and Nart, formulated in terms of indices.

</details>


### [138] [Deterministic and Efficient Ideal Arithmetic via Two-Element Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26993)
*Qi Cheng*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种确定性的多项式时间算法，用于在数域中寻找理想的二元素生成表示，该算法适用于范数与指数互素的情形，尤其覆盖了格密码中广泛使用的分圆多项式等单生成元多项式情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多数域理想的应用场景中，需要找到生成该理想的两个元素，但现有算法要么是随机化的，要么在密码学规模下不可行。因此需要一种确定性的、多项式时间的实用算法。

Method: 核心技术创新是对Dedekind判据的推广。算法要求输入理想的范数与指数 \([O_K:\mathbb{Z}[x]/f]\) 互素，适用于单生成元多项式（monogenic f(x)）情形。

Result: 提出了一个确定性的多项式时间算法，能够为满足范数与指数互素条件的理想找到二元素表示。特别地，该算法覆盖了所有单生成元多项式情形，包括格密码中常用的分圆多项式。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了理想二元素表示确定性算法的空白，在密码学应用（尤其是格密码）中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Given an ideal in a number field, it is desirable in many situations
  to find two elements that generate the ideal over the ring of the
  integers of the field. Existing algorithms are either randomized,
  or impractical at cryptographic sizes.
  In the paper, we present a deterministic
  polynomial time algorithm to find the two-element representation of
  an ideal. For a monic irreducible integral polynomial \( f(x) \),
  let \( K=\Q[x]/(f) \) be the number field, and
  \( O_K \) be the integral closure. Our algorithm works when
  the norm of the input ideal is co-prime to the index
  \( [O_K:\Z[x]/f] \). In particular, it
  handles all ideals for monogenic \( f(x) \),
  a class that includes the cyclotomic polynomials widely used in
  lattice based cryptography. A key technical ingredient in our
  result is a generalized version of Dedekind criterion.

</details>


### [139] [Averaged Fourier Estimates and Dyadic Approximation on the Cantor set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27034)
*Prasuna Bandi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Cantor集上dyadic逼近的零一律问题，给出了关于‖2^n x‖ < n^{-τ}无限多解的Hausdorff测度结果：当τ > 2-γ时测度为0，当τ < (1-γ)/2时测度为1，推进了Velani猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进Velani关于middle-third Cantor集中dyadic逼近零一律的猜想，确定临界指数阈值。

Method: 利用Cantor集的自然概率测度μ，通过分形几何和测度论方法分析‖2^n x‖的逼近性质，分别在τ > 2-γ和τ < (1-γ)/2两个区域建立测度为0和1的结论。

Result: 当τ > 2-γ时，μ{x∈C: ‖2^n x‖ < n^{-τ} i.o.}=0；当τ < (1-γ)/2时，该测度为1。

Conclusion: 给出了Cantor集上dyadic逼近零一律问题的新进展，但完整临界阈值尚未完全确定，仍存在gap需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Let $C$ be the middle-third Cantor set and let $μ$ be the natural Cantor probability measure. Let \[ γ=\frac{\log2}{\log3}. \] The two main results of this paper are \[ μ\{x\in C:\|2^n x\|<n^{-τ}\text{ for infinitely many }n\}=0 \qquad \text{ for } τ>2-γ. \] and \[ μ\{x\in C:\|2^n x\|<n^{-τ}\text{ for infinitely many }n\}=1 \qquad \text{ for } τ<\frac{1-γ}{2}. \] These results give new progress toward Velani's conjecture on zero-one law for dyadic approximation in the middle-third Cantor set.

</details>


### [140] [Adelic Line Bundles, Arithmetic Positivity and Diophantine Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27116)
*Xinyi Yuan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文系统阐述了拟射影簇上adelic线丛的基础理论、算术正性及其在等分布定理和一致Bogomolov猜想中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为adelic线丛的算术正性理论提供系统性的入门介绍，并展示其在解决等分布问题和一致Bogomolov猜想中的关键作用。

Method: 采用阐释性（expository）写作方式，系统梳理adelic线丛在拟射影簇上的基本定义与性质，阐述算术正性概念，并以此为基础推导等分布定理和一致Bogomolov猜想。

Result: 建立了adelic线丛的算术正性理论框架，并成功应用于等分布定理和一致Bogomolov猜想的证明。

Conclusion: Adelic线丛的算术正性理论是理解和证明算术几何中重要定理（如等分布定理和Bogomolov猜想）的有力工具。

Abstract: This article is an expository account of the basics of adelic line bundles on quasi-projective varieties, arithmetic positivity of adelic line bundles, and applications of positivity to an equidistribution theorem and the uniform Bogomolov conjecture.

</details>


### [141] [Quantitativity in the Mordell Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27129)
*Xinyi Yuan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文综述了Mordell猜想及其均匀性问题的定量版本，重点介绍了Yu-Yuan-Zhou最近的工作成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mordell猜想断言在数域上亏格至少为2的光滑射影曲线上仅有有限多个有理点。均匀Mordell问题进一步寻求该猜想中有理点数量的合适上界，此前已由Vojta、Dimitrov-Habegger-Gao和Kuhne的工作解决。本文旨在介绍由Yu-Yuan-Zhou近期工作证明的该均匀性问题的定量版本。

Method: 本文为一篇综述性文章，系统梳理了Mordell猜想的发展脉络，并重点介绍了Yu-Yuan-Zhou在均匀Mordell问题定量版本上的最新工作。

Result: 综述了均匀Mordell问题的定量版本，该结果由Yu-Yuan-Zhou近期证明。

Conclusion: Yu-Yuan-Zhou的工作为均匀Mordell问题提供了定量上界，推进了该领域的研究。

Abstract: The Mordell conjecture asserts that there are only finitely many rational points on a smooth projective curve of genus at least two over a number field. The uniform Mordell problem asks for suitable upper bounds on the number of rational points in the Mordell conjecture, and has been solved by combining works of Vojta, Dimitrov--Habegger--Gao and Kuhne. In this survey, we will introduce a quantitative version of the uniformity problem proved by the recent work of Yu--Yuan--Zhou.

</details>


### [142] [Determining Newforms via various relations among Fourier Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27190)
*Arvind Kumar,Moni Kumari,Prabhat Kumar Mishra*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个新形式傅里叶系数之间的算术关系如何用于确定底层形式。对于扭曲不等价的非CM新形式，获得了素数次幂归一化傅里叶系数之差、积和比的分布定量结果。作为应用，推导了重数一的定量精炼，并建立了Gafni-Thorner-Wong定理的密度一类似物。进一步证明，对于扭曲不等价的新形式，傅里叶系数之比在实数线上定量稠密，为通过系数比分布确定新形式提供了新准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究两个新形式的傅里叶系数之间的算术关系能否以及如何用于唯一确定这两个新形式本身，这是模形式理论中重数一问题的深化。

Method: 通过研究扭曲不等价非CM新形式的归一化傅里叶系数在素数次幂上的差、积和比的分布，运用解析数论和模形式理论的技术获得定量结果。

Result: 获得了系数差、积和比的分布定量结果；建立了重数一定量精炼；证明了Gafni-Thorner-Wong定理的密度一类似物；证明了系数比在实数线上定量稠密。

Conclusion: 傅里叶系数之间的算术关系（特别是比值）可以用于唯一确定新形式，系数比的定量稠密性提供了判断新形式的新准则。

Abstract: In this article, we investigate how arithmetic relations among the Fourier coefficients of two newforms can be used to determine the underlying forms. For pairs of twist-inequivalent non-CM newforms, we obtain quantitative results on the distribution of differences, products, and ratios of normalized Fourier coefficients at prime powers. As applications, we derive quantitative refinements of multiplicity one and establish density one analogues of a theorem of Gafni--Thorner--Wong. We further show that, for twist-inequivalent newforms, the ratios of Fourier coefficients are quantitatively dense in the real line, which provides a new criterion for determining newforms through the distribution of their coefficient ratios.

</details>


### [143] [The Bogomolov Property through Galois Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27203)
*Lea Terracini*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文综述了Bogomolov性质在伽罗瓦表示框架下的研究成果，重点介绍了利用局部p进信息（特别是Sen定理）建立的新准则，并将其应用于模表示和大局部像的表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bogomolov性质是数论中一个重要的无限域性质，最初作为Northcott性质的弱化引入，并与Lehmer猜想密切相关。本文旨在通过伽罗瓦表示的视角重新诠释经典结果，并将其推广到更广泛的几何与非几何起源的表示中。

Method: 采用综述性方法，系统梳理了Bogomolov性质在伽罗瓦表示框架下的研究技术与成果。特别地，引入了Sen关于全分歧p进李扩张的定理，以此建立证明Bogomolov性质的新准则，并将这些准则应用于具有大局部像的伽罗瓦表示。

Result: 给出了模表示方面的若干新结果，展示了如何利用局部p进信息来判定Bogomolov性质，并成功将准则应用于具有大局部像的伽罗瓦表示。

Conclusion: 伽罗瓦表示框架为研究Bogomolov性质提供了统一且有力的视角，Sen定理等局部p进工具在此框架中发挥了关键作用。该工作基于与Francesco Amoroso、Andrea Conti和Pietro Piras的合作研究。

Abstract: The Bogomolov property \B for an algebraic extension of \(\QQ\) asserts the existence of a uniform positive lower bound for the absolute logarithmic Weil height outside the group of roots of unity. Originally introduced as a weakening of Northcott's property and closely related to Lehmer's conjecture, it has been established for several natural classes of infinite extensions, including abelian extensions and fields generated by torsion points of elliptic curves defined over the rationals.
  Given a Galois representation of an absolute Galois group, one can associate with it the algebraic extension fixed by its kernel and ask whether this extension has property \B. This point of view allows one to reinterpret classical results, and to generalize them to other Galois representations, both of geometric and non-geometric origin.
  This expository paper gives a survey of the techniques and results in this framework. We present some results on modular representations, with a particular focus on the role of local \(p\)-adic information. We explain how Sen's theorem on totally ramified \(p\)-adic Lie extensions enters the proof of new criteria for the Bogomolov property, and how these criteria apply to representations with large local image.
  This contribution is based on joint work with Francesco Amoroso, Andrea Conti, and Pietro Piras.

</details>


### [144] [A direct proof of Mono-Rolen-Stumpenhusen and new constructions via the Maass raising operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27212)
*Kathrin Bringmann,Ben Kane*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用微分算子（Maass提升算子）给出了Mono、Rolen和Stumpenhusen主要结果的直接概念性证明，将函数ω_{k+1,D}实现为二次型Poincaré级数f_{k,D}在Maass提升算子下的像，从而自然解释了其模性和Laplace特征值性质，并进一步研究了更一般局部Maass形式在Maass升降算子下的像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Mono、Rolen和Stumpenhusen的主要结果提供一个更直接、概念性的证明，避免原作中可能复杂的推导过程，并自然揭示函数ω_{k+1,D}的模性和Laplace特征值性质的来源。

Method: 利用Maass提升算子（Maass raising operator）将二次型Poincaré级数f_{k,D}映射到函数ω_{k+1,D}，通过微分算子的作用建立两者之间的显式联系。进一步研究更一般的局部Maass形式在Maass提升和降低算子下的行为。

Result: 成功实现了ω_{k+1,D}作为f_{k,D}在Maass提升算子下的像，自然导出了其模变换性质和Laplace特征值；并将结果推广到更一般的局部Maass形式在Maass升降算子作用下的情形。

Conclusion: 微分算子视角为理解相关特殊函数的模性质和谱性质提供了统一且自然的框架，并具有向更广泛Maass形式类推广的潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we give a direct conceptual proof of the main result of Mono, Rolen, and Stumpenhusen, using differential operators. More precisely, we realize their functions $ω_{k+1,D}$ as images of the quadratic form Poincaré series $f_{k,D}$ under the Maass raising operator. This perspective gives a natural explanation for the modularity and Laplace eigenvalue prop erties of $ω_{k+1,D}$. We further extend these results by investigating the images of more general local Maass forms under the Maass raising and lowering operators.

</details>


### [145] [Weighted Fruit Diophantine Equations and Hyperelliptic Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27322)
*Jewel Mahajan,Apeksha Sanghi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究加权果型丢番图方程 $ax^{d} - c(m^{2}y^{2}+n^{2}z^{2}) + xyz - b = 0$，推广了Majumdar--Sury等人的工作。在特定参数假设下，证明了当素数 $l \equiv 3 \pmod 4$ 且 $b = a(2cmn)^{d} - l c^{s}t^{2q}$ 时，方程无整数解（除 $x$ 模 $4l$ 的某些剩余类外）。类似结论在 $l$ 替换为其奇次幂时也成立。对 $l=-1$ 给出了不可解性结果。应用于小 $l$ 值（如3,7,11,19）时显式确定了例外剩余类。$l=3$ 时得到完全不可解性。还考虑了更一般的变体方程，建立了正解的上界，并通过超椭圆曲线和Grant的Nagell-Lutz定理类比将不可解性转化为有理挠点结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广并统一Majumdar--Sury、Vaishya--Sharma和Prakash--Chakraborty等前期工作，研究一类加权三次丢番图方程的可解性条件，深化对特定形式丢番图方程整数解存在性的理解。

Method: 通过模 $4l$ 的剩余类分析、参数化构造 $b$ 的特殊形式、超椭圆曲线与Nagell-Lutz定理类比、正解有界性分析等数论与代数几何工具。

Result: 在 $l \equiv 3 \pmod 4$ 且 $b$ 取特定形式下，方程除某些 $x$ 模 $4l$ 的剩余类外无整数解；$l=3$ 时完全不可解；$l=-1$ 时也有不可解性结果；对更一般变体方程给出了不可解性与正解上界。

Conclusion: 该文系统推广了前期结果，建立了更一般的不可解性准则，并通过超椭圆曲线联系到有理挠点问题，为后续丢番图方程研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study the weighted fruit Diophantine equation $ax^{d} - c\bigl(m^{2}y^{2}+n^{2}z^{2}\bigr) + xyz - b = 0$, generalising previous work by Majumdar--Sury, Vaishya--Sharma, and Prakash--Chakraborty. Subject to specific hypotheses on the parameters, our main result shows that for any prime $l \equiv 3 \pmod 4$ and $b = a (2 c m n)^{d} - l\, c^{s}t^{2q}$, the above equation has no integer solutions except for certain residue classes of $x$ modulo $4l$. An analogous result also holds when $l$ is replaced by an odd power of $l$ in the definition of $b$. We prove some insolvability results for $l=-1$. By applying the main result to the small values of $l$, such as $l \in \{3, 7, 11, 19\}$, we explicitly determine the exceptional residue classes outside of which the equation has no solutions. In particular, for $l = 3$, this yields complete insolvability, and weakening these hypotheses still yields non-existence results, though with specific coprimality restrictions on any possible solutions. We also consider a more general variant of the above Diophantine equation and provide some insolvability results. Subsequently, we establish bounds for the positive solutions of the aforementioned equation. Finally, by associating a family of hyperelliptic curves with the equation under consideration and applying Grant's analogue of the Nagell--Lutz theorem, we translate these insolvability results into results about their rational torsion points.

</details>


### [146] [Amplified moments of the Riemann zeta function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27323)
*Benjamin Durkan,Timothy Page*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了黎曼zeta函数两片放大二次和四次矩的渐近公式，并由此推导出zeta函数多个联合矩的无条件有效下界，这些下界与Keating-Snaith等的随机矩阵理论猜想高度吻合。特别地，证明了zeta函数第六矩的无条件下界M_3(T)≥(34.1+o(1))c_3T(log T)^9，并改进了Soundararajan在Lindelöf假设下得到的一些下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 获得黎曼zeta函数高阶矩的无条件下界，验证并逼近Keating-Snaith等提出的随机矩阵理论猜想，并消除对Lindelöf假设的依赖。

Method: 建立两片放大二次和四次矩的渐近公式，利用这些渐近公式推导联合矩的下界估计。

Result: 获得了第六矩的无条件下界M_3(T)≥(34.1+o(1))c_3T(log T)^9，改进了Soundararajan依赖Lindelöf假设的结果，并对所有整数联合矩给出了有效的下界。

Conclusion: 所得下界与随机矩阵理论的预测高度一致，且无需依赖未证明的猜想（如Lindelöf假设）。

Abstract: We establish asymptotic formulae for two-piece amplified second and fourth moments of the Riemann zeta function. As applications, we obtain unconditional effective lower bounds for several joint moments of zeta which are in strong agreement with the conjectures of Keating--Wei and Keating--Snaith. In particular, we prove an unconditional lower bound for the sixth moment of zeta $M_3(T)\geq(34.1+o(1))c_3T(\log T)^9$. We further improve some of the lower bounds obtained by Soundararajan, removing the assumption of the Lindelőf Hypothesis, and we obtain effective lower bounds for all joint integer moments of zeta consistent with the predictions of random matrix theory.

</details>

### [147] [A unified cell-merge algorithm for generating diverse Voronoi diagrams and new tessellations based on spatial chromatic model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27235)
*Weining Zhu*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于空间染色模型的细胞合并(CM)沃罗诺伊生成算法，能够通过统一数据库快速生成多种类型的沃罗诺伊图，无需为每种类型开发特定算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沃罗诺伊图类型繁多（如普通VD、球面VD、加权VD、高阶有序VD等），每种类型需要各自的生成算法，缺乏统一高效的生成方法。为解决这一问题，提出一种能快速生成多种VD的统一算法。

Method: 基于空间染色模型，提出细胞合并(CM)沃罗诺伊生成算法，通过从统一数据库中检索和合并细胞来生成各类沃罗诺伊图。

Result: CM算法能够快速生成多种多样的VD，无需为每种VD开发特定算法；此外还能生成一些新型空间镶嵌图，如竞争强度图和细胞对图。

Conclusion: CM沃罗诺伊算法在需要频繁计算和分析多种沃罗诺伊图的场景（如基于位置的空间分析）中具有应用价值，并能拓展生成经典VD之外的新型空间镶嵌结构。

Abstract: As a type of spatial tessellation model and an important spatial structure of computational geometry, Voronoi diagrams (VDs) are widely used in many fields. Due to differences in generation spaces, types of spatial entities, distance metrics, and relationships between entities and Voronoi regions, Voronoi diagrams vary into many types, such as the ordinary VD, VD on spheres, VD for linear entities, weighted VD, and ordered higher-order VD. These VDs also have their own generation algorithms. In this study, we propose a new cell-merge (CM) Voronoi generation algorithm based on the spatial chromatic model. The advantage of the CM algorithm is that it can quickly generate diverse VDs by retrieving and merging cells from a unified database, without requiring the development of specific algorithms for each VD. The CM Voronoi algorithm can be particularly applied in cases where a variety of Voronoi diagrams are frequently required for computation and analysis, such as in location-based spatial analysis. Furthermore, the proposed CM method can also generate some new types of spatial tessellations, such as competition intensity and couple-cell diagrams which are different from classical VDs.

</details>
