### [148] [On Support Cardinality for Discrete Schrödinger Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26149)
*Linjun Li*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究离散薛定谔方程非平凡解的最小支撑集大小，提出了一个降维原理：支撑集大小的下界随维度增加而不会减小，从而将低维结果自动推广到高维，并在四维中得到了近乎最优的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究离散薛定谔方程的非平凡解能有多稀疏，即非零点数的最小可能值。

Method: 提出了一个降维原理（dimension-reduction principle）：支撑集的最小可能大小不会随维度增加而减小。该原理使得在d-1维中证明的任何下界自动适用于d维。

Result: 在四维中得到了一个近乎最优的下界，与最佳已知的二维构造仅差一个对数因子。

Conclusion: 高维问题的下界可以基于低维结果通过降维原理获得，且四维的下界已接近最优。

Abstract: How sparse can a nontrivial solution of a discrete Schrödinger equation be? In this note we study Dirichlet solutions on a finite $d$-dimensional lattice box, allowing an arbitrary real potential, and measure sparsity by the number of lattice sites at which the solution is nonzero (assuming it is nonzero at the origin). Our main result is a dimension-reduction principle: the minimal possible support size cannot decrease when the dimension increases. Consequently, any lower bound proved in dimension $d-1$ automatically yields the same lower bound in dimension $d$. As an application, we obtain a nearly sharp lower bound in four dimensions, matching the best-known two-dimensional constructions up to a logarithmic factor.

</details>


### [149] [The Colored Hofstadter Butterfly as a Many-Body Quantum Hall Phase Diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26256)
*Giovanna Marcelli,Tadahiro Miyao,Domenico Monaco,Stefan Teufel,Marius Wesle*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于一大类弱相互作用晶格费米子系统，彩色Hofstadter蝴蝶图具有多体解释。通过结合准绝热延拓与磁扰动的分母无关估计，作者在任意磁通量下构造了无限大相互作用系统的局部唯一带隙基态，并证明了多体缺标定理：霍尔电导率在连通的一致带隙区域内是常数且量子化的，从而将Hofstadter蝴蝶图中的整数颜色解释为相互作用量子霍尔相的霍尔电导率标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非相互作用的Hofstadter蝴蝶图推广到相互作用系统中，证明其颜色（整数）在弱相互作用下仍然保持为霍尔电导率的量子化标签。

Method: 结合准绝热延拓（quasi-adiabatic continuation）与分母无关的磁扰动估计（denominator-independent magnetic perturbation estimates），同时处理了有理数和无理数磁通量情形。

Result: 在三维参数空间（磁场b、化学势μ、相互作用强度λ）中构造了无限体积相互作用系统的局部唯一带隙基态，并证明了多体缺标定理：霍尔电导率在连通一致带隙区域内为常数且量子化，满足2πσ^H ∈ ℤ。

Conclusion: 非相互作用的Hofstadter蝴蝶图的整数颜色在弱相互作用下仍然保持为相互作用量子霍尔相的霍尔电导率标签，即该蝴蝶图具有多体解释。

Abstract: We prove that the colored Hofstadter butterfly has a many-body interpretation for a broad class of weakly interacting lattice fermion systems. Starting from a spectral gap of a Hofstadter-like one-particle Hamiltonian at arbitrary magnetic flux $b$, we construct an open region in the three-dimensional parameter space $(b,μ,λ)$ of magnetic field, chemical potential, and interaction strength on which the infinite-volume interacting system has locally unique gapped ground states. The construction combines quasi-adiabatic continuation in the interaction strength with denominator-independent magnetic perturbation estimates, and therefore covers both commensurate and incommensurate fluxes, where no finite magnetic unit cell exists. On connected uniformly gapped regions meeting the non-interacting plane $λ=0$, we prove a many-body gap-labeling theorem: the Hall conductivity appearing in the macroscopic Ohm's law is constant and quantized, satisfying $2πσ^{\mathrm{H}}\in\mathbb{Z}$. Thus the integer colors of the non-interacting Hofstadter butterfly persist as Hall-conductivity labels of interacting quantum Hall phases.

</details>


### [150] [A new formulation of metriplectic dynamics with an application to quasigeostrophic ocean modeling with advected quantities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26388)
*Francisco J. Beron-Vera,Erwin Luesink*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的度量辛动力学公式，通过两个斜对称括号的乘积构造度量辛四括号，并将其应用于广义二维准地转上层海洋模型中引入不可逆性，确保内能守恒和熵生成的 thermodynamics 一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有度量辛动力学框架在引入不可逆性时缺乏灵活性，特别是在带有平流量的二维准地转海洋模型中，需要一种既能保证热力学定律（内能守恒与熵增）又能够灵活指定耗散形式的通用方法。

Method: 通过两个斜对称括号的乘积构造度量辛四括号，建立了通用的度量辛动力学公式；将该公式应用于含有平移量的广义二维准地转上层海洋模型（以热QG模型为特例），实现不可逆性的引入。

Result: 所构建的动力学既能确保内能守恒和熵的产生（符合热力学第一、第二定律），又提供了从近乎物质守恒的位涡度到实际二维QG海洋模型中强迫与耗散表示的灵活不可逆性指定范围。

Conclusion: 该度量辛动力学公式为在二维QG海洋建模中灵活引入不可逆性提供了理论框架，在保证热力学一致性的前提下，覆盖了从理想化到实际耗散场景的广泛适用性。

Abstract: A general formulation of metriplectic dynamics is presented, where the metriplectic four-bracket is constructed by multiplying two skew-symmetric brackets. The new formulation is then used to introduce irreversibility in a generalized two-dimensional (2D) quasigeostrophic (QG) upper-ocean model involving advected quantities, with the thermal QG model as a special case. By construction, the resulting dynamics ensure the conservation of internal energy and the generation of entropy, in accordance with the first and second laws of thermodynamics. Our metriplectic dynamics formulation allows for a flexible specification of irreversibility, ranging from a type that results in nearly material conservation of potential vorticity to the representation of realistic forcing and dissipation in 2D QG ocean modeling with advected quantities.

</details>


### [151] [Weak-Strong Resurgence Duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26637)
*Gerald V. Dunne,Ayssar I. Farah*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了当弱耦合展开的收敛半径为零而强耦合展开的收敛半径为无穷大（或反之）时，两者之间存在显式的复苏对偶（resurgent duality）。通过Airy和Pearcey灾变积分进行说明，并应用于两个物理实例：零维标量φ⁴理论中Dyson-Schwinger方程的弱/强耦合展开，以及Gross-Neveu模型中的热核迹的短/长时间展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 补充已有复苏对偶理论的空白——之前的研究分别处理了收敛展开具有有限收敛半径或两个展开均具有零收敛半径的情形，而本文针对一个展开收敛半径为零、另一个为无穷大的新情形建立对偶关系。

Method: 利用复苏函数（resurgent function）形式框架，通过Airy积分和Pearcey积分这一对具有不同解析性质的灾变积分，从数学上证明弱耦合与强耦合展开之间的对偶关系，并将该框架应用于两个具体的物理系统。

Result: 成功建立了收敛半径为零与收敛半径为无穷大的展开之间的复苏对偶，并通过Airy和Pearcey积分给出了显式验证；在φ⁴理论和Gross-Neveu模型中均验证了该对偶的存在性。

Conclusion: 这扩展了复苏对偶的适用范围，表明即使收敛性质截然不同的展开之间仍存在深刻的数学结构关联，为处理非微扰效应和瞬子求和提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We show that there is an explicit resurgent duality between weak and strong coupling expansions when one of the expansions has zero radius of convergence and the other has infinite radius of convergence. This complements the situation where the convergent expansion has finite radius of convergence, or when both expansions have zero radius of convergence. We illustrate this phenomenon for the Airy and Pearcey catastrophe integrals, and we apply it to two physical examples: the weak and strong coupling expansions of Dyson-Schwinger equations in zero-dimensional scalar $φ^4$ theories, and the short and long time expansions of the heat kernel trace for the fluctuation operator of the kink-antikink crystal saddle configuration in the Gross-Neveu model.

</details>


### [152] [Wave-Particle Decomposition for Kinetic Equations I: Theory and Numerics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26915)
*Chang Liu,Kun Xu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于波粒分解的动力学松弛方程求解框架，通过局部演化时间尺度与动力学视界将分布函数分解为解析累积的波动分量和纯动力学粒子分量，构建了跨努森谱统一的波粒系统，并设计了守恒宏微观离散方法，数值验证了其多尺度精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一气体动力学波粒方法（UGKWP）采用全局时间步分裂，未在偏微分方程（PDE）层面定义波粒分解，缺乏严格的连续理论基础。本文旨在建立一种PDE层面的、基于局部演化时间尺度与弛豫时间之比的波粒分解框架，以实现跨努森谱统一的连续波粒系统。

Method: 利用特征积分解将分布函数分解为解析累积的波分量与纯动力学粒子分量；粒子分量由超越局部动力学视界的无碰撞输运定义。构建由无源总守恒律、波动方程和粒子方程组成的统一波粒系统。波算子通过Chapman-Enskog展开得到依赖视界的Euler和Navier-Stokes通量。在算法层面，采用守恒宏微观方法离散：总守恒变量通过有限体积更新，使用Navier-Stokes气体动力学波通量与粒子通量（由确定性离散坐标S_N法或Monte Carlo法计算）之和。

Result: 形式分析建立了渐近保持连续极限、稀薄区一致性以及活动动力学自由度的跨区域自适应缩放特性。一、二、三维数值测试验证了框架的精度、多尺度能力与计算效率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的PDE层面波粒分解框架克服了UGKWP方法全局时间步分裂的局限性，实现了跨努森谱统一的、具备渐近保持特性的多尺度动力学求解方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a wave-particle decomposition (WPD) for kinetic relaxation equations, formulated around a local evolution timescale and its associated kinetic horizon. By leveraging the characteristic integral solution, we decompose the distribution function into an analytically accumulated wave component and a purely kinetic particle component. The latter is defined by the collisionless transport that survives beyond a prescribed local domain of influence, termed the horizon. This continuous formulation yields a unified wave-particle system valid across the entire Knudsen spectrum, comprising a source-free total conservation law, a wave equation, and a particle equation. The wave operator admits a Chapman--Enskog expansion, whose moments yield Euler and Navier--Stokes fluxes with horizon-dependent coefficients, while the particle equation governs the remaining non-equilibrium kinetic transport.
  At the algorithmic level, this system is discretized by a conservative macro-micro method. The total conservative variables are advanced by a finite-volume update using the sum of a Navier--Stokes gas-kinetic wave flux and a particle flux computed by either a deterministic discrete-ordinate $(S_N)$ method or a Monte Carlo representation. Unlike the global time-step splitting in the unified gas-kinetic wave-particle (UGKWP) method, the present partition is defined at the PDE level and governed by the local ratio of evolution timescale to relaxation time. The particle component is therefore a fractional kinetic population generated by the collisionless factor of the integral solution. Formal analysis establishes the asymptotic-preserving continuum limit, rarefied-regime consistency, and regime-adaptive scaling of active kinetic degrees of freedom. Numerical tests in one, two, and three dimensions validate the accuracy, multiscale capability, and efficiency of the framework.

</details>


### [153] [Asymptotic solutions: glancing trajectories, Lagrangian singularities, and Bessel cylinders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26962)
*Ilya Bogaevskii,Michel Rouleux*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对半经典分布$f_h$微局域化在Lagrangian流形$\Lambda_0$上的方程$(\widehat{H}-E_0) u_h = f_h$，在$E_0$是$H|_{\Lambda_0}$的临界值时，通过寻找Hamilton-Jacobi方程解投影到相空间能量上的最简Lagrangian奇点的规范形，来近似渐近解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当$E_0$为$H|_{\Lambda_0}$的临界值时，标准WKB方法失效，需要研究奇点附近的渐近行为。

Method: 寻找Hamilton-Jacobi方程解的Lagrangian奇点（投影到空间-能量上）的规范形，并利用该规范形构造渐近解。

Result: 得到了最简Lagrangian奇点的规范形，并基于此给出了$\widehat{H}-E_0)u_h=f_h$的渐近解近似。

Conclusion: 该方法扩展了半经典分析的适用范围，能够处理$E_0$为临界值时的奇点情形。

Abstract: We find a normal form for the simplest Lagrangian singularity appearing as the projection onto the space-energy of
  a solution to the Hamilton-Jacobi equation. Using this normal form we approximate asymptotic solutions of an equation
  $(\widehat{H}-E_0) \, u_h = f_h$ with a semiclassical distribution $f_h$ microlocalized on a Lagrangian manifold $Λ_0$
  when $E_0$ is a critical value of the restriction $H|_{Λ_0}$.

</details>


### [154] [The Ising Model Coupled to 2D Gravity: Critical Partition Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27125)
*Maurice Duits,Nathan Hayford,Seung-Yeop Lee*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，在特定双尺度极限下，含四次相互作用的2-矩阵模型配分函数对数的微分收敛到(3,4)弦方程的τ-函数的微分，从而证实了随机表面上临界伊辛模型收敛到(3,4)拓扑极小模型这一经典论断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Douglas-Shenker、Brézin-Kazakov以及Gross-Migdal等人关于随机表面上临界伊辛模型收敛到(3,4)拓扑极小模型的理论预言。

Method: 对与双正交多项式族相关的Riemann-Hilbert问题进行最速下降分析，并处理局部参量构造中出现的匹配问题的新特征。

Result: 成功证明了在双尺度极限下，2-矩阵模型配分函数对数的微分收敛到(3,4)弦方程的τ-函数的微分。

Conclusion: 该结果从数学上严格确认了随机表面上的临界伊辛模型与(3,4)拓扑极小模型之间的等价性。

Abstract: We prove that the differential of the log of the partition function for the $2$-matrix model with quartic interactions converges in a certain double-scaling regime to the differential of the $\boldsymbolτ$-function for the $(3,4)$ string equation. This confirms the convergence of the critical Ising model on random surfaces to the $(3,4)$ topological minimal model, which was stated in the works of Douglas and Shenker, Brézin and Kazakov, and Gross and Migdal. Our analysis is based on a steepest-descent analysis of a Riemann-Hilbert problem associated to a family of biorthogonal polynomials. New features in the matching problem in the construction of local parametrices appear.

</details>


### [155] [Geometric bulk-edge correspondence for $\mathbb{Z}_2$-topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27318)
*Alexis Drouot,Jacob Shapiro,Xiaowen Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明二维时间反演不变绝缘体（Kitaev表AII类）在弯曲界面下的几何体边对应关系：边界系统的Z_2边缘指数等于两个体相Z_2指数差与边界及测量区域相关几何交点数模2的乘积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立弯曲界面下拓扑绝缘体体边对应的严格几何表述，将此前针对霍尔绝缘体的弯曲界面连接公式推广到Z_2拓扑绝缘体（AII类）。

Method: 采用Z_2版本的弯曲界面连接公式证明方法，类比文献[DZ24]中霍尔绝缘体的论证框架。

Result: 弯曲界面下体边对应成立：边缘Z_2指数 = (体相Z_2指数差) × (几何交点数) mod 2。

Conclusion: 为二维时间反演不变绝缘体的拓扑相提供了弯曲几何界面下的严格体边对应关系，拓展了平直界面体边对应的经典结论。

Abstract: Fermionic time-reversal-invariant insulators in two dimensions--class AII in the Kitaev table--come in two topological phases. These phases are characterized by a $\mathbb{Z}_2$-valued invariant, the Fu-Kane-Mele index. We prove a geometric bulk-edge correspondence for curved interfaces: if two such insulators occupy complementary regions separated by a curved boundary, then the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ edge index of the interface system is the product, modulo two, of the difference of the two bulk $\mathbb{Z}_2$ indices and a geometric intersection number associated with the boundary and the measurement region. The argument is a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ analogue of the curved-interface connection formula proved for Hall insulators in \cite{DZ24}.

</details>


### [156] [$\mathrm{W}^*$-algebraic Integration Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27366)
*Jan Głowacki,Yui Kuramochi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义了W*-代数值函数的积分，将其视为空间张量积中的元素，建立了通用积分域、商空间，并证明了积分映射是完全正映射、*-同态或等距映射的条件，以及莱布尼茨规则和富比尼定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典测度论中的函数积分推广到算子代数框架下，建立W*-代数值函数关于POVM的积分理论，为量子信息、非交换概率论等提供严格的数学基础。

Method: 通过空间张量积定义积分$\int f \otimes d\mathsf{E} \in \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S} \bar{\otimes} \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{R}$；引入$B_b(Σ,\mathcal{F},\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S})$作为通用积分域，并构造商空间$L^\infty_\mathsf{E}(Σ,\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S})$；利用Stinespring分解通过Naimark dilation推导完全正性；建立算子值莱布尼茨规则和富比尼定理。

Result: 当$(\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{R})_*$可分时，$L^\infty_\mathsf{E}(Σ,\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S}) \cong \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S} \bar{\otimes} L^\infty_\mathsf{E}(Σ)$是W*-代数；积分映射是忠实正规单位正CP映射，对PVM是*-同态，对局部化POVM是等距映射；积分映射可分解为$\boldsymbol{1}_{\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S}} \hat{\otimes} Φ_\mathsf{E}$。

Conclusion: 建立了W*-代数值函数的非交换积分理论，实现了经典积分性质（线性、保范、莱布尼茨规则、富比尼定理）在算子代数框架下的推广。

Abstract: Given a pair of $\mathrm{W}^*$-algebras $(\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S},\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{R})$ with $(\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S})_*$ separable, a measurable space $(Σ, \mathcal{F})$ and a POVM $\mathsf{E}: \mathcal{F} \to \mathcal{E}(\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{R})$, the integral of a function $f: Σ\to \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S}$ is defined as an element of the spatial tensor product $\int f \otimes d\mathsf{E} \in \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S} \bar{\otimes} \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{R}$. The space $B_b(Σ,\mathcal{F},\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S})$ of uniformly bounded ultraweakly measurable functions is the universal domain of integration; once $\mathsf{E}$ is fixed it refines to the quotient $L^\infty_\mathsf{E}(Σ,\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S}) = B_b(Σ,\mathcal{F},\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S})/\mathcal{N}_\mathsf{E}$ by $\mathsf{E}$-null functions. When $(\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{R})_*$ is also separable, $L^\infty_\mathsf{E}(Σ,\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S}) \cong \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S} \bar{\otimes} L^\infty_\mathsf{E}(Σ)$ is a $\mathrm{W}^*$-algebra. The integration map is a faithful normal unital completely positive (CP) map, a $*$-homomorphism for PVMs and an isometry for localizable POVMs. It can be identified with the spatial tensor product $\boldsymbol{1}_{\mathcal{M}_\mathcal{S}} \hat{\otimes} Φ_\mathsf{E}$ where $Φ_\mathsf{E}: L^\infty_\mathsf{E}(Σ) \to \mathcal{M}_\mathcal{R}$ is the faithful normal positive map corresponding to $\mathsf{E}$. Complete positivity of integration maps is derived from Stinespring factorization through Naimark dilation. We establish an operator-valued Leibniz rule and Fubini theorem.

</details>

### [157] [Sensitivity of evolutionary entropy in Lefkovitch matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26170)
*Henrqiue M. Oliveira*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文发展了不可约Lefkovitch矩阵中进化熵的完整敏感性理论，将Demetrius-Gundlach-Ziehe的熵敏感性理论从年龄结构推广到一般阶段结构种群，推导了显式闭式表达式，并给出了熵的自然分解为转换和保留分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将进化熵的敏感性理论从年龄结构Leslie矩阵扩展到更一般的阶段结构Lefkovitch矩阵，以适用于更广泛的实证人口学模型。

Method: 利用Perron-Frobenius表示关联马尔可夫链，推导了平稳分布、世代时间、进化熵及其对生育率、转换率和保留率参数的偏导数的显式闭式表达式，并将结果表达为人口学系数、Perron特征向量、主特征值和生殖潜力的函数。

Result: 获得了进化熵的自然分解为转换分量和保留分量，阐明了阶段结构种群中人口学不确定性产生的不同机制，并证明了该理论可特化为开放群组Leslie矩阵。

Conclusion: 该工作将熵敏感性理论从年龄结构推广到一般阶段结构种群，为比较人口学分析、扰动研究、人口学稳健性和生活史策略研究提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Evolutionary entropy, introduced by Demetrius, is a demographic invariant that quantifies the temporal organization of structured populations. Explicit sensitivity expressions for this quantity were derived by Demetrius, Gundlach and Ziehe for age-structured Leslie matrices, establishing the foundations of entropy-based perturbation theory.
  In this paper we develop a complete sensitivity theory for evolutionary entropy in irreducible Lefkovitch matrices. Using the Perron--Frobenius representation of the associated Markov chain, we derive explicit closed-form expressions for the stationary distribution, generation time, evolutionary entropy and its partial derivatives with respect to fertility, transition and retention parameters. The resulting identities are expressed directly in terms of demographic coefficients, Perron eigenvectors, the dominant eigenvalue and the reproductive potential.
  The entropy representation obtained here gives a natural decomposition into transition and retention components and clarifies the distinct mechanisms through which demographic uncertainty is generated in stage-structured populations. We further show that the theory specializes immediately to open-group Leslie matrices, a class that has been shown to comprise a large fraction of empirical demographic models.
  The results extend the entropy sensitivity theory of Demetrius--Gundlach--Ziehe from age-structured to general stage-structured populations and provide practical tools for comparative demographic analysis, perturbation studies, demographic robustness, and the investigation of life-history strategies. Several biological examples are presented, illustrating how entropy decomposition and sensitivity analysis reveal complementary aspects of population organization.

</details>


### [158] [An Investigation of Additional Food Models with Generalised Functional Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26340)
*Urvashi Verma,Kanishka Goyal,Saptarshi Biswas,James I. Lathrop,Rana D. Parshad*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类通用额外食物捕食者-猎物模型，建立了共存平衡点全局稳定的条件，证明了Holling IV型功能响应下存在余维3的Bogdanov-Takens分岔，并通过化学反应网络理论揭示了额外食物引入增加反应网络缺陷度与复杂分岔之间的潜在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 额外食物源常用于提升捕食者对害虫种群的控制效果，但其模型因非对称结构导致动力学分析困难，因此需要系统研究这类模型的稳定性与分岔行为。

Method: 采用动力系统稳定性分析与分岔理论，结合确定性化学反应网络理论，对一类通用额外食物模型进行数学分析。

Result: 建立了共存平衡点全局稳定的条件；在Holling IV型功能响应下发现了余维3的Bogdanov-Takens分岔；发现额外食物引入会提高反应网络缺陷度，且高缺陷度与复杂分岔存在关联。

Conclusion: 额外食物的引入会显著改变捕食-被捕食系统的动力学行为，且反应网络缺陷度可能成为预测系统复杂动力学行为的重要指标。

Abstract: Additional food sources are often used to improve the effectiveness of predators in controlling pest populations. However, the non-symmetric structure of additional food predator-prey models can cause certain aspects of their dynamics challenging to analyze. In this work, we study a general class of additional food models and establish conditions under which the coexistence equilibrium is globally stable. We then focus on a Holling type IV functional response with AF and show the existence of a Bogdanov-Takens bifurcation of codimension 3. We also study these models through the lens of deterministic chemical reaction network theory. Our analysis shows that the introduction of additional food increases the deficiency of the underlying reaction network and suggests a possible link between higher deficiency and complex bifurcations.

</details>


### [159] [Smoothly Time-Varying Continuous Time Markov Chains in Phylogenetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26402)
*Pratyusa Datta,Philippe Lemey,Marc A. Suchard*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种样条时钟模型，通过使用非齐次连续时间马尔可夫链和三次B样条基展开来建模随时间变化的进化速率，并通过高斯-勒让德数值积分和高斯马尔可夫随机场先验实现高效计算与平滑性约束。模拟和实证研究表明，该模型比竞争模型更准确地恢复了时变速率信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化速率估计依赖于采样时间框架，这给从分子序列数据重建进化历史带来了根本性挑战。现有方法难以灵活应对速率随时间变化的情况，需要一种更精确的建模方法。

Method: 使用非齐次连续时间马尔可夫链（ICTMC）建模系统发育分支上的序列替换过程；将对数变换的速率参数化为时间的三次B样条基展开（样条时钟模型）；使用高斯-勒让德求积法高效近似似然计算中的速率函数积分；为样条系数施加高斯马尔可夫随机场先验以强制平滑性。

Result: 模拟研究表明，样条时钟模型比竞争时钟模型能更准确地恢复真实时变速率，并具有更窄的置信区间。应用于泡沫病毒的进化速率和SARS-CoV-2在欧洲的空间扩散速率，均恢复了强烈的时变信号。

Conclusion: 样条时钟模型是一种灵活且准确的方法，能够有效捕捉分子进化速率随时间变化的动态特征，在进化生物学和传染病研究中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: The dependence of evolutionary rate estimates on the timeframe of sampling poses a fundamental challenge for reconstructing evolutionary histories from molecular sequence data, which is central to evolutionary biology and infectious disease research. We present a novel and flexible approach to accommodate time-varying evolutionary rates by modeling the sequence substitution process using inhomogeneous continuous-time Markov chains (ICTMCs) acting along the branches of the phylogeny, and parameterizing the log transformed rate as a smooth function of time using a cubic B-spline basis expansion. Following the parlance of phylogenetics that refers to rates of molecular substitutions as molecular clocks, we call this a spline clock model. Integrals of the rate function over all branches, required for likelihood evaluation, are approximated efficiently using Gauss-Legendre quadrature, and smoothness is enforced by assigning a Gaussian Markov random field prior to the spline coefficients. Through a simulation study, we demonstrate that the spline clock model recovers the true time-varying rates more accurately and with tighter credible intervals than competing clock models. We apply the spline clock model to examine the evolutionary rate of foamy virus and the rate of spatial diffusion of SARS-CoV-2 across Europe, recovering strong time-varying signal in both settings.

</details>


### [160] [Semialgebraic Conditions for Identifying Triangles in Phylogenetic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26673)
*Bryan Currie,Aviva K. Englander,Jose A. Esparza-Lozano,Elizabeth Gross,Max Hill,Colby Long,Devon Olds,Kawika O'Connor,Udani Ranasinghe,Christin Sum*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文对带有Jukes-Cantor位点替换过程且无共祖的三个三叶系统发育网络模型进行了完整的半代数描述，证明尽管这些带有嵌入三角形的网络模型在代数上不可区分，但它们并不相同，也不可识别（或一般不可识别），并揭示了杂交信号随时间衰减直至无法识别三角边方向的生物学解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于模型的系统发育网络推断中网络参数的可识别性问题，特别是嵌入三角形边方向难以甚至无法确定这一长期困扰该领域的核心问题。

Method: 对三个三叶Jukes-Cantor系统发育网络模型（含嵌入三角形）进行完整的半代数描述，通过分析模型间的交集与集合差的全维区域性质来研究可识别性。

Result: 任意一对模型之间的交集和集合差都是位点模式概率分布空间的全维区域；尽管代数上不可区分，但这些网络模型既不相同也不可识别（或一般不可识别）。

Conclusion: 杂交事件产生的信号可能立即被检测到，但会随时间衰减，直至无法识别网络中三角形边的方向。

Abstract: An important consideration for a model-based method of phylogenetic network inference is the identifiability of the network parameter of the model. A recurring theme in previous works exploring this issue is that it is often difficult to identify the orientation of edges in a triangle of the network. In fact, it has been shown that for some models it is impossible to determine the orientation of triangle edges utilizing the standard algebraic technique of phylogenetic invariants. In this work, we consider one such model with a Jukes-Cantor site-substitution process and no coalescence. We give a complete semialgebraic description of three, 3-leaf Jukes-Cantor phylogenetic network models with embedded triangles. By describing these base cases, we resolve several questions about the identifiability of networks with embedded triangles. We show that for any pair of models, the intersection and set differences of the models are full-dimensional regions of the space of site-pattern probability distributions. Thus, despite being algebraically indistinguishable, these network models are not identical, nor are they identifiable (or generically identifiable). Our results also yield a straightforward biological interpretation--that the signal from a hybridization event may be immediately detectable but decays over time until it is impossible to identify the orientation of edges in the triangle of a network.

</details>


### [161] [On-farm management strategies for reducing H5N1 transmission in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26674)
*Oliver Eales,Scott Ison,Rachael Gibney,Zoe Vogels,James M. McCaw,Freya M. Shearer*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文建立H5N1在奶牛场传播的数学模型，发现将奶牛分成“挤奶队列”的预防性措施能有效降低疫情暴发风险，并表明频繁的散装牛奶样本检测有助于早期发现和及时干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b于2024年初传入美国奶牛场，导致多地疫情暴发，严重威胁动物健康、造成经济损失并危及人类健康。然而不同传播途径的相对贡献尚不明确，亟需量化风险因素并制定有效干预措施。

Method: 建立描述H5N1在奶牛场上传播动力学的数学模型，考虑多种可能的传播途径，模拟不同干预策略的效果。

Result: 将奶牛划分为“挤奶队列”（即分群饲养并按固定顺序挤奶）能显著降低暴发风险，且不受主要传播途径影响；该策略在预防性实施时最有效；新引入的奶牛应置于最后一个挤奶队列中；频繁的散装牛奶样本检测（如每周一次）可快速发现疫情并及时实施反应性干预。

Conclusion: 研究结果为制定奶牛场H5N1疫情防控管理指南提供了科学依据，支持通过队列管理和定期监测来有效应对疫情。

Abstract: Introductions of H5N1 clade 2.3.4.4b into dairy cattle have resulted in outbreaks on dairy farms across the United States since early-2024. Outbreaks have significant consequences for animal health, result in economic losses for the dairy industry, and pose a threat to human health. Though the relative contributions of different on-farm transmission pathways remain a key uncertainty, a major route is considered to be through repeated contamination of milking stalls (i.e. the equipment and area where an individual cow is milked) due to the milking of infected animals. Here we develop mathematical models of H5N1 transmission dynamics on dairy farms, considering multiple possible transmission pathways, and identify factors that contribute to outbreak risk and on-farm interventions for mitigating risk. In particular, we demonstrate that dividing cattle into 'milking cohorts', with cohorts kept in separate pens or paddocks and milked in the same order every day, would be highly effective at mitigating outbreaks irrespective of the dominant transmission pathway. Cohorting cattle is most effective when implemented pre-emptively (i.e. before an outbreak) and when newly introduced cattle are kept in the final milking cohort. Additionally, we demonstrate that frequent bulk milk sample testing (e.g. weekly) would enable the rapid detection of outbreaks and implementation of reactive interventions (or scaling up of existing interventions). Our findings can support the development of management guidelines for effectively responding to H5N1 outbreaks in dairy cattle.

</details>


### [162] [Hyperiax and Phylogenetic Inference from Shape Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26974)
*Gefan Yang,Marcus Teller,Christy Hipsley,Rasmus Nielsen,Stefan Sommer*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Hyperiax，一个基于JAX的开源库，用于高效执行树遍历算法和消息传递，支持BFFG框架在高维形态性状（如蝴蝶翅膀和鸟喙）的系统发育推断，在850和696个节点的树上实现了比以前更高分辨率的形状数据分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维形态性状的系统发育推断需要同时处理形状数据的非线性几何和系统发育树结构，BFFG框架能解决该问题但缺乏高效实现，限制了实际应用。

Method: 开发了Hyperiax开源库，利用JAX实现树遍历算法和消息传递，支持BFFG框架所需的操作，包括离散时间和随机微分方程模型的推断。

Result: 在850个节点的蝴蝶翅膀（118个二维地标点）和696个节点的鸟喙（79个三维地标点）数据集上成功实现了参数推断和祖先状态重建，规模远超以往。

Conclusion: Hyperiax通过高效实现使BFFG框架能够应用于更大规模、更高分辨率的系统发育形态推断问题，拓展了该方法的实用性。

Abstract: Phylogenetic inference on high-dimensional morphological traits requires algorithms that account for both the nonlinear geometry of the shape data and the phylogenetic tree structure. The Backward Filtering Forward Guiding (BFFG) framework provides smoothing for nonlinear stochastic processes on trees and enables inference of parameters and ancestral states. As practical adoption has been limited by a lack of efficient implementations, we present Hyperiax, an open-source library for tree traversal algorithms and message passing using JAX, designed particularly to support operations needed for BFFG. Hyperiax enables efficient execution of operations on trees with large numbers of nodes and, coupled with the BFFG-specific operations, this allows efficient inference in both discrete-time and stochastic differential equation models. Concretely, we demonstrate that Hyperiax enables parameter inference and ancestral reconstruction for butterfly wing shapes represented by landmarks in two dimensions, and analyses of avian beaks from landmarks in three dimensions. Both cases demonstrate application of BFFG on two substantially larger phylogenetic trees with 850 and 696 nodes with higher resolution shape data (118 two-dimensional landmarks and 79 three-dimensional landmarks, specifically) than previously possible.

</details>
